# Dhan Kuber - Complete Website with Admin Panel

A modern, full-stack Next.js 14 application for displaying Dhan Kuber results with a secure admin panel for managing charts and results.

## Features

- **Public Website**: Display results for Gali, Desawar, Faridabad, Ghaziabad and other markets
- **Admin Panel**: Secure authentication with role-based access control
- **Chart Management**: Add, edit, delete, and highlight chart results
- **Date/Year Management**: Dynamic year/month/date filtering with historical data
- **Responsive Design**: Mobile-first approach with Tailwind CSS
- **TypeScript**: Full type safety throughout the application
- **MongoDB**: Database with Mongoose ODM
- **Server Actions**: Secure server-side operations

## Tech Stack

- **Framework**: Next.js 14 (App Router)
- **Language**: TypeScript
- **Styling**: Tailwind CSS
- **Database**: MongoDB with Mongoose
- **Authentication**: JWT with HTTP-only cookies (iron-session)
- **Validation**: Zod
- **Icons**: Lucide React
- **Forms**: React Hook Form (with server actions)

## Project Structure

```
src/
├── app/
│   ├── layout.tsx              # Root layout
│   ├── page.tsx                # Home page
│   ├── globals.css             # Global styles
│   ├── admin/
│   │   ├── login/page.tsx      # Admin login
│   │   ├── dashboard/page.tsx  # Admin dashboard
│   │   └── charts/
│   │       ├── page.tsx        # Charts list
│   │       ├── add/page.tsx    # Add chart
│   │       └── edit/[id]/page.tsx # Edit chart
│   └── api/
│       ├── admin/              # Admin API routes
│       ├── charts/             # Public charts API
│       └── contact/            # Contact form API
├── components/
│   ├── layout/                 # Header, Footer, Navigation
│   ├── home/                   # Home page components
│   ├── admin/                  # Admin panel components
│   └── shared/                 # Reusable UI components
├── lib/
│   ├── mongodb.ts              # MongoDB connection
│   ├── auth.ts                 # Authentication utilities
│   └── validations.ts          # Zod schemas
├── models/
│   ├── Admin.ts                # Admin schema
│   └── Chart.ts                # Chart schema
├── actions/
│   ├── admin.actions.ts        # Admin server actions
│   └── chart.actions.ts        # Chart server actions
└── types/                      # TypeScript types
```

## Getting Started

### Prerequisites

- Node.js 18+
- MongoDB (local or Atlas)
- npm or yarn

### Installation

1. Clone the repository
2. Install dependencies:

   ```bash
   npm install
   ```

3. Configure environment variables:

   ```bash
   cp .env.example .env.local
   ```

   Edit `.env.local` with your configuration:

   ```env
   MONGODB_URI=mongodb://localhost:27017/Dhan-Kuber
   ADMIN_SECRET_KEY=your-super-secret-key-min-32-chars-long
   ADMIN_COOKIE_PASSWORD=your-32-char-password-for-iron-session
   NEXT_PUBLIC_WHATSAPP_NUMBER=91XXXXXXXXXX
   ```

4. Initialize database and create admin user:

   ```bash
   npm run db:push
   npm run seed
   ```

5. Start development server:

   ```bash
   npm run dev
   ```

6. Open http://localhost:3000

### Admin Access

- URL: http://localhost:3000/admin/login
- Default credentials:
  - Username: `admin`
  - Password: `admin123`
- **Important**: Change password after first login!

## Available Scripts

| Command           | Description               |
| ----------------- | ------------------------- |
| `npm run dev`     | Start development server  |
| `npm run build`   | Build for production      |
| `npm run start`   | Start production server   |
| `npm run lint`    | Run ESLint                |
| `npm run seed`    | Create initial admin user |
| `npm run db:push` | Create database indexes   |

## Admin Panel Features

### Dashboard

- Total charts count
- Active results count
- Today's entries
- Latest added charts

### Charts Management

- **Add Chart**: Create new chart entries with all fields
- **Edit Chart**: Modify existing chart data
- **Delete Chart**: Remove charts with confirmation
- **Search & Filter**: By name, date, month, year, status
- **Pagination**: For large datasets
- **Highlight**: Mark important results
- **Active/Inactive**: Toggle visibility

### Date/Year Management

- Dynamic year options from database
- Future year support (2025, 2026, 2027+)
- Month/date filtering based on available data
- Historical data preservation

## Security Features

- Server-side authentication on all admin routes
- HTTP-only, Secure, SameSite cookies
- Bcrypt password hashing (12 rounds)
- Rate limiting on login attempts
- Account lockout after failed attempts
- Role-based authorization (super_admin, admin, editor)
- CSRF protection via SameSite cookies
- Input validation with Zod
- No client-side route protection only

## Deployment

### Vercel (Recommended)

1. Push to GitHub
2. Import project in Vercel
3. Add environment variables
4. Deploy

### Docker

```dockerfile
FROM node:18-alpine
WORKDIR /app
COPY package*.json ./
RUN npm ci --only=production
COPY . .
RUN npm run build
EXPOSE 3000
CMD ["npm", "start"]
```

### Environment Variables for Production

```env
MONGODB_URI=mongodb+srv://...
ADMIN_SECRET_KEY=generate-secure-32-char-key
ADMIN_COOKIE_PASSWORD=generate-32-char-password
NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL=https://yourdomain.com
SESSION_SECURE=true
```

## Customization

### Markets/Charts

Edit `COMMON_CHARTS` array in `src/components/admin/ChartForm.tsx`

### Colors

Modify `tailwind.config.ts` for custom color palette

### Announcements

Update default announcements in `src/app/page.tsx`

## API Endpoints

### Public

- `GET /api/charts` - Get charts with filters
- `GET /api/charts/years` - Get available years
- `GET /api/charts/months?year=` - Get months for year
- `GET /api/charts/dates?year=&month=` - Get dates for month/year
- `POST /api/contact` - Contact form

### Admin (Protected)

- `POST /api/admin/login` - Admin login
- `POST /api/admin/logout` - Admin logout
- `GET /api/admin/me` - Get current admin
- `GET /api/admin/stats` - Dashboard stats
- `GET /api/admin/charts` - List charts with pagination
- `POST /api/admin/charts` - Create chart
- `GET /api/admin/charts/[id]` - Get chart
- `PUT /api/admin/charts/[id]` - Update chart
- `DELETE /api/admin/charts/[id]` - Delete chart
- `POST /api/admin/charts/[id]/highlight` - Toggle highlight
- `POST /api/admin/charts/[id]/active` - Toggle active
- `GET /api/admin/charts/years` - Get years
- `GET /api/admin/charts/months?year=` - Get months
- `GET /api/admin/charts/dates?year=&month=` - get dates

FILE: scripts\db-push.ts
============================================================

import * as dotenv from "dotenv";
import path from "path";
dotenv.config({ path: path.resolve(process.cwd(), ".env.local") });

import mongoose from "mongoose";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Admin from "@/models/Admin";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";

async function pushDB() {
  try {
    await connectDB();

    // Create indexes - handle conflicts gracefully
    try {
      await Admin.syncIndexes();
    } catch (e: any) {
      if (e.code === 86 || e.codeName === "IndexKeySpecsConflict") {
        console.log("Admin indexes already exist (conflict handled)");
      } else {
        throw e;
      }
    }
    
    try {
      await Chart.syncIndexes();
    } catch (e: any) {
      if (e.code === 86 || e.codeName === "IndexKeySpecsConflict") {
        console.log("Chart indexes already exist (conflict handled)");
      } else {
        throw e;
      }
    }

    console.log("Database indexes created/verified successfully");
    process.exit(0);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error pushing database:", error);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

pushDB();

import * as dotenv from "dotenv";
import path from "path";
dotenv.config({ path: path.resolve(process.cwd(), ".env.local") });
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";

async function main() {
  try {
    await connectDB();
    const res = await Chart.deleteMany({
      chartName: { $in: [/^\s*gali\s*$/i, /^\s*desawar\s*$/i] },
    });
    console.log("Deleted Gali/Desawar chart documents:", res.deletedCount);
    process.exit(0);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Removal failed:", error);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}
main();

FILE: scripts\reset-admin-password.ts
============================================================

/**
 * Reset an admin password directly in MongoDB.
 * Usage: npm run reset:admin -- <username> <newPassword>
 * Target a specific DB by setting MONGODB_URI in the shell first
 * (dotenv never overrides an env var that is already set).
 */
import dotenv from "dotenv";
dotenv.config({ path: ".env.local" });

import mongoose from "mongoose";
import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";

async function main() {
  const uri = process.env.MONGODB_URI;
  const username = (process.argv[2] || "admin").trim();
  const newPassword = process.argv[3];

  if (!uri) {
    console.error("❌ MONGODB_URI is not set.");
    process.exit(1);
  }
  if (!newPassword || newPassword.length < 8) {
    console.error("Usage: npm run reset:admin -- <username> <newPassword  (8+ characters)>");
    process.exit(1);
  }

  await mongoose.connect(uri);
  console.log(`Connected → host: ${mongoose.connection.host} | database: ${mongoose.connection.name}`);

  const db = mongoose.connection.db!;
  const admins = db.collection("admins");
  const existing = await admins.findOne({ username });

  if (!existing) {
    const list = await admins.find({}, { projection: { username: 1, role: 1 } }).toArray();
    console.error(`❌ No admin named "${username}" in this database.`);
    console.error("   Admins present:", list.map((a) => a.username));
    console.error("   Collections:", (await db.listCollections().toArray()).map((c) => c.name));
    await mongoose.disconnect();
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const hash = await bcrypt.hash(newPassword, 12);
  const res = await admins.updateOne(
    { _id: existing._id },
    { $set: { password: hash, isActive: true, updatedAt: new Date() } }
  );

  console.log(`✅ Password reset for "${username}" — matched ${res.matchedCount}, modified ${res.modifiedCount}`);
  console.log("   Log in on the site that uses THIS database, then change it again in Settings if you like.");
  await mongoose.disconnect();
}

main().catch(async (err) => {
  console.error("Reset failed:", err);
  try { await mongoose.disconnect(); } catch {}
  process.exit(1);
});

FILE: scripts\seed-admin.ts
============================================================

import * as dotenv from "dotenv";
import path from "path";
dotenv.config({ path: path.resolve(process.cwd(), ".env.local") });
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Admin from "@/models/Admin";

async function seedAdmin() {
  const username = process.env.ADMIN_USERNAME || "admin";
  const password = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "admin123";
  const email = process.env.ADMIN_EMAIL || `${username}@localhost`;
  try {
    await connectDB();
    const existingAdmin = await Admin.findOne({ username });
    if (existingAdmin) {
      // Plain password here — the model's pre-save hook hashes it exactly once
      existingAdmin.passwordHash = password;
      existingAdmin.isActive = true;
      existingAdmin.loginAttempts = 0;
      existingAdmin.lockUntil = undefined;
      await existingAdmin.save();
      console.log(`Admin "${username}" password reset from .env.local successfully`);
      process.exit(0);
    }
    const admin = await Admin.create({
      username,
      email,
      passwordHash: password,
      role: "super_admin",
      isActive: true,
    });
    console.log("Admin user created successfully:", {
      id: (admin._id as mongoose.Types.ObjectId).toString(),
      username: admin.username,
      email: admin.email,
      role: admin.role,
    });
    process.exit(0);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error seeding admin:", error);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}
seedAdmin();

FILE: scripts\unhighlight-defaults.ts
============================================================

import * as dotenv from "dotenv";
import path from "path";
dotenv.config({ path: path.resolve(process.cwd(), ".env.local") });
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { DEFAULT_MARKETS } from "@/lib/markets";

/** One-off: remove the auto-seeded highlights so nothing is yellow unless the admin set it. */
async function main() {
  try {
    await connectDB();
    const res = await Chart.updateMany(
      { chartName: { $in: DEFAULT_MARKETS }, isHighlighted: true },
      { $set: { isHighlighted: false } }
    );
    console.log("Un-highlighted seeded default rows:", res.modifiedCount);
    process.exit(0);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Un-highlight failed:", error);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}
main();

============================================================
FILE: src\actions\admin.actions.ts
============================================================

"use server";

import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Admin from "@/models/Admin";
import { hashPassword, verifyPassword, createToken, verifyToken, clearSession, ROLES } from "@/lib/auth";
import { loginSchema, adminSchema, updateAdminSchema } from "@/lib/validations";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";

export interface AdminData {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  email: string;
  role: string;
  isActive: boolean;
  lastLoginAt?: Date;
  createdAt: Date;
}

export interface ActionResult<T = unknown> {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
  data?: T;
  errors?: Record<string, string[]>;
}

async function rateLimitCheck(identifier: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const maxAttempts = parseInt(process.env.RATE_LIMIT_MAX_REQUESTS || "5");
  const windowMs = parseInt(process.env.RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS || "900000");

  const key = `ratelimit:${identifier}`;
  const now = Date.now();

  if (typeof globalThis !== "undefined") {
    const store = (globalThis as { ratelimit?: Map<string, { count: number; resetAt: number }> }).ratelimit || new Map();
    (globalThis as { ratelimit?: Map<string, { count: number; resetAt: number }> }).ratelimit = store;

    const record = store.get(key);

    if (!record || record.resetAt < now) {
      store.set(key, { count: 1, resetAt: now + windowMs });
      return true;
    }

    if (record.count >= maxAttempts) {
      return false;
    }

    record.count += 1;
    return true;
  }

  return true;
}

export async function loginAction(formData: FormData): Promise<ActionResult> {
  const identifier = `login:${formData.get("username")}`;

  if (!(await rateLimitCheck(identifier))) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Too many login attempts. Please try again later.",
    };
  }

  const validation = loginSchema.safeParse({
    username: formData.get("username"),
    password: formData.get("password"),
  });

  if (!validation.success) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Invalid input",
      errors: validation.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  await connectDB();

  const admin = await Admin.findOne({
    $or: [{ username: validation.data.username }, { email: validation.data.username }],
  }).select("+passwordHash");

  if (!admin) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Invalid username or password",
    };
  }

  if (!admin.isActive) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Account is deactivated. Contact administrator.",
    };
  }

  if (admin.isLocked) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Account temporarily locked due to too many failed attempts. Try again in 15 minutes.",
    };
  }

  const isValid = await verifyPassword(validation.data.password, admin.passwordHash);

  if (!isValid) {
    await admin.incrementLoginAttempts();
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Invalid username or password",
    };
  }

  await admin.resetLoginAttempts();
  admin.lastLoginAt = new Date();
  await admin.save();

  const token = await createToken({
    id: (admin._id as mongoose.Types.ObjectId).toString(),
    username: admin.username,
    email: admin.email,
    role: admin.role,
  });

  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  cookieStore.set("admin_session", token, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
    sameSite: "lax",
    maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24,
    path: "/",
  });

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Login successful",
    data: { redirect: "/admin/dashboard" },
  };
}

export async function logoutAction(): Promise<ActionResult> {
  await clearSession();
  redirect("/admin/login");
}

export async function getCurrentAdmin(): Promise<ActionResult<AdminData>> {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;

  if (!token) {
    return { success: false, message: "Not authenticated" };
  }

  const payload = await verifyToken(token);

  if (!payload) {
    return { success: false, message: "Invalid session" };
  }

  await connectDB();
  const admin = await Admin.findById(payload.id).select("-passwordHash").lean();

  if (!admin || !admin.isActive) {
    return { success: false, message: "Admin not found or inactive" };
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Admin found",
    data: {
      id: admin._id.toString(),
      username: admin.username,
      email: admin.email,
      role: admin.role,
      isActive: admin.isActive,
      lastLoginAt: admin.lastLoginAt,
      createdAt: admin.createdAt,
    },
  };
}

export async function createAdminAction(formData: FormData): Promise<ActionResult> {
  const session = await getCurrentAdmin();
  if (!session.success || !["super_admin"].includes(session.data?.role || "")) {
    return { success: false, message: "Unauthorized" };
  }

  const validation = adminSchema.safeParse({
    username: formData.get("username"),
    email: formData.get("email"),
    password: formData.get("password"),
    role: formData.get("role"),
  });

  if (!validation.success) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Invalid input",
      errors: validation.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  await connectDB();

  const existing = await Admin.findOne({
    $or: [{ username: validation.data.username }, { email: validation.data.email }],
  });

  if (existing) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Username or email already exists",
    };
  }

  const passwordHash = await hashPassword(validation.data.password);

  const admin = await Admin.create({
    ...validation.data,
    passwordHash,
  });

  revalidatePath("/admin/dashboard");

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Admin created successfully",
    data: {
      id: (admin._id as mongoose.Types.ObjectId).toString(),
      username: admin.username,
      email: admin.email,
      role: admin.role,
    },
  };
}

export async function updateAdminAction(adminId: string, formData: FormData): Promise<ActionResult> {
  const session = await getCurrentAdmin();
  if (!session.success || !["super_admin"].includes(session.data?.role || "")) {
    return { success: false, message: "Unauthorized" };
  }

  const validation = updateAdminSchema.safeParse({
    username: formData.get("username"),
    email: formData.get("email"),
    password: formData.get("password") || undefined,
    role: formData.get("role"),
    isActive: formData.get("isActive") === "true",
  });

  if (!validation.success) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Invalid input",
      errors: validation.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  await connectDB();

  const admin = await Admin.findById(adminId);
  if (!admin) {
    return { success: false, message: "Admin not found" };
  }

  if ((admin._id as mongoose.Types.ObjectId).toString() === session.data?.id && validation.data.isActive === false) {
    return { success: false, message: "Cannot deactivate your own account" };
  }

  const updateData: Record<string, unknown> = {};

  if (validation.data.username) updateData.username = validation.data.username;
  if (validation.data.email) updateData.email = validation.data.email;
  if (validation.data.role) updateData.role = validation.data.role;
  if (typeof validation.data.isActive === "boolean") updateData.isActive = validation.data.isActive;

  if (validation.data.password) {
    updateData.passwordHash = await hashPassword(validation.data.password);
  }

  const updated = await Admin.findByIdAndUpdate(adminId, updateData, { new: true }).select("-passwordHash");

  revalidatePath("/admin/dashboard");

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Admin updated successfully",
    data: updated,
  };
}

export async function deleteAdminAction(adminId: string): Promise<ActionResult> {
  const session = await getCurrentAdmin();
  if (!session.success || !["super_admin"].includes(session.data?.role || "")) {
    return { success: false, message: "Unauthorized" };
  }

  if (adminId === session.data?.id) {
    return { success: false, message: "Cannot delete your own account" };
  }

  await connectDB();

  const admin = await Admin.findByIdAndDelete(adminId);
  if (!admin) {
    return { success: false, message: "Admin not found" };
  }

  revalidatePath("/admin/dashboard");

  return { success: true, message: "Admin deleted successfully" };
}

export async function getAdminsAction(): Promise<ActionResult> {
  const session = await getCurrentAdmin();
  if (!session.success || !["super_admin", "admin"].includes(session.data?.role || "")) {
    return { success: false, message: "Unauthorized" };
  }

  await connectDB();

  const admins = await Admin.find().select("-passwordHash").sort({ createdAt: -1 }).lean();

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Admins fetched",
    data: admins.map((a) => ({
      id: a._id.toString(),
      username: a.username,
      email: a.email,
      role: a.role,
      isActive: a.isActive,
      lastLoginAt: a.lastLoginAt,
      createdAt: a.createdAt,
    })),
  };
}

FILE: src\actions\chart.actions.ts

"use server";

import mongoose from "mongoose";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { chartSchema, chartUpdateSchema, chartFilterSchema } from "@/lib/validations";
import { requireAuth, requireRole } from "@/lib/auth";

export interface ActionResult<T = unknown> {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
  data?: T;
  errors?: Record<string, string[]>;
}

export interface PaginatedCharts {
  charts: Array<{
    id: string;
    chartName: string;
    result: string;
    openingResult?: string;
    closingResult?: string;
    date: number;
    month: number;
    year: number;
    displayOrder: number;
    isHighlighted: boolean;
    isActive: boolean;
    formattedDate: string;
    createdAt: Date;
    updatedAt: Date;
  }>;
  pagination: {
    page: number;
    limit: number;
    total: number;
    totalPages: number;
  };
}

export async function createChartAction(formData: FormData): Promise<ActionResult> {
  await requireRole(["super_admin", "admin", "editor"]);

  const validation = chartSchema.safeParse({
    chartName: formData.get("chartName"),
    result: formData.get("result"),
    openingResult: formData.get("openingResult") || undefined,
    closingResult: formData.get("closingResult") || undefined,
    date: formData.get("date"),
    month: formData.get("month"),
    year: formData.get("year"),
    displayOrder: formData.get("displayOrder"),
    isHighlighted: formData.get("isHighlighted") === "true",
    isActive: formData.get("isActive") === "true",
  });

  if (!validation.success) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Invalid input",
      errors: validation.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  await connectDB();

  const existing = await Chart.findOne({
    chartName: validation.data.chartName,
    date: validation.data.date,
    month: validation.data.month,
    year: validation.data.year,
  });

  if (existing) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "A chart with this name and date already exists",
    };
  }

  const chart = await Chart.create(validation.data);

  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/admin/charts");

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Chart created successfully",
    data: {
      id: (chart._id as mongoose.Types.ObjectId).toString(),
      ...chart.toObject(),
    },
  };
}

export async function updateChartAction(chartId: string, formData: FormData): Promise<ActionResult> {
  await requireRole(["super_admin", "admin", "editor"]);

  const validation = chartUpdateSchema.safeParse({
    chartName: formData.get("chartName"),
    result: formData.get("result"),
    openingResult: formData.get("openingResult") || undefined,
    closingResult: formData.get("closingResult") || undefined,
    date: formData.get("date"),
    month: formData.get("month"),
    year: formData.get("year"),
    displayOrder: formData.get("displayOrder"),
    isHighlighted: formData.get("isHighlighted") === "true",
    isActive: formData.get("isActive") === "true",
  });

  if (!validation.success) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Invalid input",
      errors: validation.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  await connectDB();

  const chart = await Chart.findById(chartId);
  if (!chart) {
    return { success: false, message: "Chart not found" };
  }

  if (validation.data.chartName || validation.data.date || validation.data.month || validation.data.year) {
    const checkName = validation.data.chartName || chart.chartName;
    const checkDate = validation.data.date || chart.date;
    const checkMonth = validation.data.month || chart.month;
    const checkYear = validation.data.year || chart.year;

    const existing = await Chart.findOne({
      _id: { $ne: chartId },
      chartName: checkName,
      date: checkDate,
      month: checkMonth,
      year: checkYear,
    });

    if (existing) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: "A chart with this name and date already exists",
      };
    }
  }

  Object.assign(chart, validation.data);
  await chart.save();

  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/admin/charts");

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Chart updated successfully",
    data: chart.toObject(),
  };
}

export async function deleteChartAction(chartId: string): Promise<ActionResult> {
  await requireRole(["super_admin", "admin"]);

  await connectDB();

  const chart = await Chart.findByIdAndDelete(chartId);
  if (!chart) {
    return { success: false, message: "Chart not found" };
  }

  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/admin/charts");

  return { success: true, message: "Chart deleted successfully" };
}

export async function getChartsAction(filters: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<ActionResult<PaginatedCharts>> {
  await requireAuth();

  const validation = chartFilterSchema.safeParse(filters);

  if (!validation.success) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: "Invalid filters",
      errors: validation.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  await connectDB();

  const { search, date, month, year, isActive, page, limit, sortBy, sortOrder } = validation.data;

  const query: Record<string, unknown> = {};

  if (search) {
    query.chartName = { $regex: search, $options: "i" };
  }
  if (date) query.date = date;
  if (month) query.month = month;
  if (year) query.year = year;
  if (isActive && isActive !== "all") query.isActive = isActive === "true";

  const sort: Record<string, 1 | -1> = { [sortBy]: sortOrder === "asc" ? 1 : -1 };

  const [charts, total] = await Promise.all([
    Chart.find(query)
      .sort(sort)
      .skip((page - 1) * limit)
      .limit(limit)
      .lean(),
    Chart.countDocuments(query),
  ]);

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Charts fetched",
    data: {
      charts: charts.map((c) => ({
        id: c._id.toString(),
        chartName: c.chartName,
        result: c.result,
        openingResult: c.openingResult,
        closingResult: c.closingResult,
        date: c.date,
        month: c.month,
        year: c.year,
        displayOrder: c.displayOrder,
        isHighlighted: c.isHighlighted,
        isActive: c.isActive,
        formattedDate: c.formattedDate,
        createdAt: c.createdAt,
        updatedAt: c.updatedAt,
      })),
      pagination: {
        page,
        limit,
        total,
        totalPages: Math.ceil(total / limit),
      },
    },
  };
}

export async function getChartByIdAction(chartId: string): Promise<ActionResult> {
  await requireAuth();

  await connectDB();

  const chart = await Chart.findById(chartId).lean();
  if (!chart) {
    return { success: false, message: "Chart not found" };
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Chart fetched",
    data: {
      id: chart._id.toString(),
      chartName: chart.chartName,
      result: chart.result,
      openingResult: chart.openingResult,
      closingResult: chart.closingResult,
      date: chart.date,
      month: chart.month,
      year: chart.year,
      displayOrder: chart.displayOrder,
      isHighlighted: chart.isHighlighted,
      isActive: chart.isActive,
      formattedDate: chart.formattedDate,
      createdAt: chart.createdAt,
      updatedAt: chart.updatedAt,
    },
  };
}

export async function getPublicChartsAction(params: {
  year?: number;
  month?: number;
  date?: number;
  chartName?: string;
}): Promise<ActionResult> {
  await connectDB();

  const query: Record<string, unknown> = { isActive: true };

  if (params.year) query.year = params.year;
  if (params.month) query.month = params.month;
  if (params.date) query.date = params.date;
  if (params.chartName) query.chartName = params.chartName;

  const charts = await Chart.find(query)
    .sort({ displayOrder: 1, chartName: 1 })
    .lean();

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Charts fetched",
    data: charts.map((c) => ({
      id: c._id.toString(),
      chartName: c.chartName,
      result: c.result,
      openingResult: c.openingResult,
      closingResult: c.closingResult,
      date: c.date,
      month: c.month,
      year: c.year,
      displayOrder: c.displayOrder,
      isHighlighted: c.isHighlighted,
      isActive: c.isActive,
      formattedDate: c.formattedDate,
    })),
  };
}

export async function getAvailableYearsAction(): Promise<ActionResult<number[]>> {
  await connectDB();

  const years = await Chart.distinct("year").sort();
  const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
  const allYears = Array.from(new Set([...years, currentYear, currentYear + 1, currentYear + 2])).sort((a, b) => b - a);

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Years fetched",
    data: allYears,
  };
}

export async function getAvailableMonthsAction(year: number): Promise<ActionResult<number[]>> {
  await connectDB();

  const months = await Chart.distinct("month", { year }).sort();
  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Months fetched",
    data: months,
  };
}

export async function getAvailableDatesAction(year: number, month: number): Promise<ActionResult<number[]>> {
  await connectDB();

  const dates = await Chart.distinct("date", { year, month }).sort();
  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Dates fetched",
    data: dates,
  };
}

export async function getDashboardStatsAction(): Promise<ActionResult> {
  await requireAuth();

  await connectDB();

  const today = new Date();
  const todayStart = new Date(today.getFullYear(), today.getMonth(), today.getDate());
  const todayEnd = new Date(today.getFullYear(), today.getMonth(), today.getDate() + 1);

        const d = today.getDate();
    const m = today.getMonth() + 1;
    const y = today.getFullYear();
    const [totalCharts, totalResults, todaysEntries, latestCharts] = await Promise.all([
      // Total Charts → today's tables only (matches the Charts page default view)
      Chart.countDocuments({ date: d, month: m, year: y }),
      // Active Results → today's tables that are Active
      Chart.countDocuments({ date: d, month: m, year: y, isActive: true }),
      // Today's Entries → today's tables added/edited today (never > Total Charts)
      Chart.countDocuments({
        date: d,
        month: m,
        year: y,
        $or: [
          { createdAt: { $gte: todayStart, $lt: todayEnd } },
          { updatedAt: { $gte: todayStart, $lt: todayEnd } },
        ],
      }),
      Chart.find()
        .sort({ updatedAt: -1 })
        .limit(5)
        .lean(),
    ]);

  return {
    success: true,
    message: "Stats fetched",
    data: {
      totalCharts,
      totalResults,
      todaysEntries,
      latestCharts: latestCharts.map((c) => ({
        id: c._id.toString(),
        chartName: c.chartName,
        result: c.result,
        date: c.date,
        month: c.month,
        year: c.year,
        createdAt: c.createdAt,
      })),
    },
  };
}

export async function toggleHighlightAction(chartId: string): Promise<ActionResult> {
  await requireRole(["super_admin", "admin", "editor"]);

  await connectDB();

  const chart = await Chart.findById(chartId);
  if (!chart) {
    return { success: false, message: "Chart not found" };
  }

  chart.isHighlighted = !chart.isHighlighted;
  await chart.save();

  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/admin/charts");

  return {
    success: true,
    message: `Chart ${chart.isHighlighted ? "highlighted" : "unhighlighted"}`,
    data: { isHighlighted: chart.isHighlighted },
  };
}

export async function toggleActiveAction(chartId: string): Promise<ActionResult> {
  await requireRole(["super_admin", "admin", "editor"]);

  await connectDB();

  const chart = await Chart.findById(chartId);
  if (!chart) {
    return { success: false, message: "Chart not found" };
  }

  chart.isActive = !chart.isActive;
  await chart.save();

  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/admin/charts");

  return {
    success: true,
    message: `Chart ${chart.isActive ? "activated" : "deactivated"}`,
    data: { isActive: chart.isActive },
  };
}

// src/app/admin/admins/page.tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { AdminSidebar } from "@/components/admin/AdminSidebar";
import { AdminHeader } from "@/components/admin/AdminHeader";
import { Loading } from "@/components/shared/Loading";
import { Button } from "@/components/shared/Button";
import { Shield, User, Mail, KeyRound } from "lucide-react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

interface AdminData {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  email: string;
  role: string;
  isActive: boolean;
  lastLoginAt?: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export default function AdminAdminsPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isSidebarOpen, setIsSidebarOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isSidebarCollapsed, setIsSidebarCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<AdminData | null>(null);
  const [allAdmins, setAllAdmins] = useState<AdminData[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchData = async () => {
      try {
        // Fetch current logged-in user
        const meRes = await fetch("/api/admin/me");
        if (meRes.ok) {
          const meData = await meRes.json();
          if (meData.success) setCurrentUser(meData.data);
          else router.push("/admin/login");
        } else {
          router.push("/admin/login");
        }

        // Fetch all admins
        const adminsRes = await fetch("/api/admin/admins");
        if (adminsRes.ok) {
          const adminsData = await adminsRes.json();
          if (adminsData.success) setAllAdmins(adminsData.data);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(err);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    };
    fetchData();
  }, [router]);

  if (isLoading || !currentUser) {
    return (
      <div className="admin-layout">
        <AdminSidebar isCollapsed={isSidebarCollapsed} onToggle={() => setIsSidebarCollapsed(!isSidebarCollapsed)} user={currentUser || undefined} />
        <div className="admin-main">
          <AdminHeader onMenuClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(true)} user={currentUser!} pageTitle="Admin Management" breadcrumbs={[{ label: "Admins" }]} />
          <div className="admin-content"><Loading text="Loading admins..." /></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="admin-layout">
      <div
        className={clsx("fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-black/50 lg:hidden transition-opacity", isSidebarOpen ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0 pointer-events-none")}
        onClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(false)}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />
      <AdminSidebar isCollapsed={isSidebarCollapsed} onToggle={() => setIsSidebarCollapsed(!isSidebarCollapsed)} user={currentUser} />
      
      <div className="admin-main">
        <AdminHeader onMenuClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(true)} user={currentUser} pageTitle="Admin Management" breadcrumbs={[{ label: "Admins" }]} />
        
        <div className="admin-content space-y-8">
          {/* Current Admin Details Card */}
          <div className="card p-6 border-2 border-primary-200 bg-primary-50/30">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
              <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-primary-100">
                <Shield className="w-6 h-6 text-primary-600" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900">Your Account Details</h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Currently logged in as <span className="font-bold text-primary-700">{currentUser.username}</span></p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200">
                <User className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" />
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">Username</p>
                  <p className="font-semibold text-gray-900">{currentUser.username}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200">
                <Mail className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" />
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">Email</p>
                  <p className="font-semibold text-gray-900">{currentUser.email}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200">
                <Shield className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" />
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">Role</p>
                  <p className="font-semibold text-gray-900 capitalize">{currentUser.role.replace("_", " ")}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200">
                <KeyRound className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" />
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">Password</p>
                  <p className="font-semibold text-gray-900">•••••••••••• (Encrypted)</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-6 p-4 bg-yellow-50 border border-yellow-200 rounded-lg flex items-start gap-3">
              <KeyRound className="w-5 h-5 text-yellow-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <div className="flex-1">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-yellow-800">Why can't I see my password?</p>
                <p className="text-sm text-yellow-700 mt-1">
                  For your security, your password is encrypted using <strong>bcrypt</strong> and stored as a one-way hash in the database. 
                  It is mathematically impossible for anyone (even the system administrators) to view or decrypt your original password. 
                  If you need to change it, please use the button below.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end">
              <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => router.push("/admin/settings")}>
                <KeyRound className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
                Change My Password
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* All Admins Table */}
          <div className="card overflow-hidden">
            <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-gray-200 bg-gray-50">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">All System Administrators</h3>
            </div>
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-sm text-left text-gray-700">
                <thead className="text-xs text-gray-600 uppercase bg-gray-50 border-b border-gray-200">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="px-6 py-3">Username</th>
                    <th className="px-6 py-3">Email</th>
                    <th className="px-6 py-3">Role</th>
                    <th className="px-6 py-3">Status</th>
                    <th className="px-6 py-3">Last Login</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {allAdmins.map((admin) => (
                    <tr key={admin.id} className={clsx("border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50", admin.id === currentUser.id && "bg-primary-50")}>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-gray-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                        {admin.username}
                        {admin.id === currentUser.id && <span className="text-xs bg-primary-100 text-primary-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">You</span>}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4">{admin.email}</td>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4 capitalize">{admin.role.replace("_", " ")}</td>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4">
                        {admin.isActive ? (
                          <span className="px-2 py-1 text-xs font-medium rounded-full bg-green-100 text-green-700">Active</span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="px-2 py-1 text-xs font-medium rounded-full bg-red-100 text-red-700">Inactive</span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4 text-gray-500">
                        {admin.lastLoginAt ? new Date(admin.lastLoginAt).toLocaleString() : "Never"}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\app\admin\charts\add\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { AdminSidebar } from "@/components/admin/AdminSidebar";
import { AdminHeader } from "@/components/admin/AdminHeader";
import { ChartForm } from "@/components/admin/ChartForm";
import { Loading } from "@/components/shared/Loading";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

export default function AdminChartAddPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const [isSidebarOpen, setIsSidebarOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isSidebarCollapsed, setIsSidebarCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [user, setUser] = useState<{ username: string; role: string } | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [availableYears, setAvailableYears] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [availableMonths, setAvailableMonths] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [availableDates, setAvailableDates] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

    // Pre-fill from ?market=&date=&month=&year= (sent by "Needs update" rows)
  const initialData = useMemo(() => {
    const market = searchParams.get("market");
    const date = parseInt(searchParams.get("date") || "", 10);
    const month = parseInt(searchParams.get("month") || "", 10);
    const year = parseInt(searchParams.get("year") || "", 10);
    return {
      ...(market ? { chartName: market } : {}),
      ...(date ? { date } : {}),
      ...(month ? { month } : {}),
      ...(year ? { year } : {}),
    };
  }, [searchParams]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchAuth = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/admin/me");
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.success) setUser(data.data);
          else router.push("/admin/login");
        } else router.push("/admin/login");
      } catch { router.push("/admin/login"); }
      finally { setIsLoading(false); }
    };
    fetchAuth();
  }, [router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchYears = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/admin/charts/years");
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.success) setAvailableYears(data.data);
        }
      } catch (err) { console.error(err); }
    };
    fetchYears();
  }, []);

  const handleSubmit = async (data: any) => {
    setIsSubmitting(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/admin/charts", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(data),
      });

      const result = await res.json();

      if (result.success) {
        router.push("/admin/charts");
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        setError(result.message || "Failed to create chart");
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError("An error occurred. Please try again.");
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="admin-layout">
        <AdminSidebar isCollapsed={isSidebarCollapsed} onToggle={() => setIsSidebarCollapsed(!isSidebarCollapsed)} user={user || undefined} />
        <div className="admin-main">
          <AdminHeader
            onMenuClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(true)}
            user={user!}
            pageTitle="Add New Chart"
            breadcrumbs={[{ label: "Charts", href: "/admin/charts" }, { label: "Add New" }]}
          />
          <div className="admin-content">
            <Loading text="Loading..." />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="admin-layout">
      <div
        className={clsx("fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-black/50 lg:hidden transition-opacity", isSidebarOpen ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0 pointer-events-none")}
        onClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(false)}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />
      <AdminSidebar
        isCollapsed={isSidebarCollapsed}
        onToggle={() => setIsSidebarCollapsed(!isSidebarCollapsed)}
        user={user || undefined}
      />
      <div className="admin-main">
        <AdminHeader
          onMenuClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(true)}
          user={user!}
          pageTitle="Add New Chart"
          breadcrumbs={[{ label: "Charts", href: "/admin/charts" }, { label: "Add New" }]}
        />
        <div className="admin-content max-w-3xl mx-auto">
          <div className="card p-6">
              <ChartForm
              initialData={initialData}
              onSubmit={handleSubmit}
              onCancel={() => router.back()}
              isLoading={isSubmitting}
              availableYears={availableYears}
              availableMonths={availableMonths}
              availableDates={availableDates}
            />
            {error && (
              <div className="mt-4 p-4 bg-danger-50 border border-danger-200 rounded-lg text-danger-700" role="alert">
                {error}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\app\admin\charts\bulk-import\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { AdminSidebar } from "@/components/admin/AdminSidebar";
import { AdminHeader } from "@/components/admin/AdminHeader";
import { Button } from "@/components/shared/Button";
import { Loading } from "@/components/shared/Loading";
import { Upload, Eye, ArrowLeft } from "lucide-react";

const SAMPLE = `Monthly Result Chart of November 2026
DATE\tDSWR\tFRBD\tGZBD\tGALI
01\tXX\t77\t63\t82
02\t64\t20\t79\t78
03\t99\t51\t15\t74`;

export default function BulkImportPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [user, setUser] = useState<{ username: string; role: string } | null>(null);
  const [isSidebarCollapsed, setIsSidebarCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<{ ok: boolean; msg: string; data?: any } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/admin/me");
        if (res.ok) {
          const d = await res.json();
          if (d.success) setUser(d.data);
          else router.push("/admin/login");
        } else router.push("/admin/login");
      } catch { router.push("/admin/login"); }
      finally { setIsLoading(false); }
    })();
  }, [router]);

  const send = async (dryRun: boolean) => {
    setBusy(true);
    setResult(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/admin/charts/bulk-import", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ text, dryRun }),
      });
      const d = await res.json();
      setResult({ ok: Boolean(d.success), msg: d.message || "Done", data: d.data });
    } catch {
      setResult({ ok: false, msg: "Network error — try again" });
    } finally { setBusy(false); }
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="admin-layout">
        <AdminSidebar isCollapsed={isSidebarCollapsed} onToggle={() => setIsSidebarCollapsed(!isSidebarCollapsed)} user={user || undefined} />
        <div className="admin-main">
          <AdminHeader user={user!} pageTitle="Bulk Import" breadcrumbs={[{ label: "Charts", href: "/admin/charts" }, { label: "Bulk Import" }]} />
          <div className="admin-content"><Loading text="Loading..." /></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="admin-layout">
      <AdminSidebar isCollapsed={isSidebarCollapsed} onToggle={() => setIsSidebarCollapsed(!isSidebarCollapsed)} user={user || undefined} />
      <div className="admin-main">
        <AdminHeader user={user!} pageTitle="Bulk Import — Special 4" breadcrumbs={[{ label: "Charts", href: "/admin/charts" }, { label: "Bulk Import" }]} />
        <div className="admin-content max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-6">

          <div className="card p-6">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Paste monthly charts (Desawar · Faridabad · Ghaziabad · Gali)</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
              Paste one month or many. Each block needs a line containing the month + year (e.g. <em>November 2026</em>) and a
              <span className="font-mono"> DATE DSWR FRBD GZBD GALI</span> header. Tab or space separated both work.
              <strong> XX</strong> and leading zeros (<span className="font-mono">02</span>) are stored exactly as pasted.
            </p>

            <textarea
              value={text}
              onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
              rows={16}
              spellCheck={false}
              placeholder={SAMPLE}
              className="input mt-4 font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed"
            />

            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
              <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => send(true)} disabled={busy || !text.trim()}>
                <Eye className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Preview
              </Button>
              <Button type="button" variant="primary" onClick={() => send(false)} loading={busy} disabled={!text.trim()}>
                <Upload className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Import
              </Button>
              <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setText(SAMPLE)} disabled={busy}>
                Load sample
              </Button>
              <Link href="/admin/charts" className="btn btn-ghost ml-auto">
                <ArrowLeft className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Back to Charts
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>

          {result && (
            <div className={`card p-6 ${result.ok ? "border-success-200 bg-success-50" : "border-danger-200 bg-danger-50"}`}>
              <p className={`font-semibold ${result.ok ? "text-success-700" : "text-danger-700"}`}>{result.msg}</p>
              {result.data?.months?.length > 0 && (
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-700">
                  Months: {result.data.months.join(", ")}
                </p>
              )}
              {result.data?.notes?.length > 0 && (
                <ul className="mt-3 list-disc pl-5 text-xs text-gray-600">
                  {result.data.notes.slice(0, 10).map((n: string, i: number) => <li key={i}>{n}</li>)}
                </ul>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { AdminSidebar } from "@/components/admin/AdminSidebar";
import { AdminHeader } from "@/components/admin/AdminHeader";
import { ChartForm } from "@/components/admin/ChartForm";
import { Loading } from "@/components/shared/Loading";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

interface ChartData {
  id: string;
  chartName: string;
  result: string;
  openingResult?: string;
  closingResult?: string;
  date: number;
  month: number;
  year: number;
  displayOrder: number;
  isHighlighted: boolean;
  isActive: boolean;
  scheduledAt?: string | null;
  drawTime?: string | null;
}

export default function AdminChartEditPage() {
  const params = useParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const chartId = params.id as string;
  const [isSidebarOpen, setIsSidebarOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isSidebarCollapsed, setIsSidebarCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [user, setUser] = useState<{ username: string; role: string } | null>(null);
  const [chart, setChart] = useState<ChartData | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [availableYears, setAvailableYears] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [availableMonths, setAvailableMonths] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [availableDates, setAvailableDates] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchAuth = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/admin/me");
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.success) setUser(data.data);
          else router.push("/admin/login");
        } else router.push("/admin/login");
      } catch { router.push("/admin/login"); }
      finally { setIsLoading(false); }
    };
    fetchAuth();
  }, [router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchChart = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/charts/${chartId}`);
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.success) {
            setChart(data.data);
            setAvailableDates([data.data.date]);
            setAvailableMonths([data.data.month]);
            setAvailableYears([data.data.year]);
          } else {
            router.push("/admin/charts");
          }
        } else {
          router.push("/admin/charts");
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(err);
        router.push("/admin/charts");
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    };

    const fetchYears = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/admin/charts/years");
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.success) setAvailableYears(data.data);
        }
      } catch (err) { console.error(err); }
    };

    fetchChart();
    fetchYears();
  }, [chartId, router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (chart?.year) {
      const fetchMonths = async () => {
        try {
          const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/charts/months?year=${chart.year}`);
          if (res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) setAvailableMonths(data.data);
          }
        } catch (err) { console.error(err); }
      };
      fetchMonths();
    }
  }, [chart?.year]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (chart?.year && chart?.month) {
      const fetchDates = async () => {
        try {
          const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/charts/dates?year=${chart.year}&month=${chart.month}`);
          if (res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success) setAvailableDates(data.data);
          }
        } catch (err) { console.error(err); }
      };
      fetchDates();
    }
  }, [chart?.year, chart?.month]);

  const handleSubmit = async (data: Omit<typeof chart, "id">) => {
    setIsSubmitting(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/charts/${chartId}`, {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(data),
      });

      const result = await res.json();

      if (result.success) {
        router.push("/admin/charts");
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        setError(result.message || "Failed to update chart");
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError("An error occurred. Please try again.");
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="admin-layout">
        <AdminSidebar isCollapsed={isSidebarCollapsed} onToggle={() => setIsSidebarCollapsed(!isSidebarCollapsed)} user={user || undefined} />
        <div className="admin-main">
          <AdminHeader
            onMenuClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(true)}
            user={user!}
            pageTitle="Edit Chart"
            breadcrumbs={[{ label: "Charts", href: "/admin/charts" }, { label: "Edit" }]}
          />
          <div className="admin-content">
            <Loading text="Loading chart..." />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!chart) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className="admin-layout">
      <div
        className={clsx("fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-black/50 lg:hidden transition-opacity", isSidebarOpen ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0 pointer-events-none")}
        onClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(false)}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />
      <AdminSidebar
        isCollapsed={isSidebarCollapsed}
        onToggle={() => setIsSidebarCollapsed(!isSidebarCollapsed)}
        user={user || undefined}
      />
      <div className="admin-main">
        <AdminHeader
          onMenuClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(true)}
          user={user!}
          pageTitle="Edit Chart"
          breadcrumbs={[{ label: "Charts", href: "/admin/charts" }, { label: "Edit" }]}
        />
        <div className="admin-content max-w-3xl mx-auto">
          <div className="card p-6">
              <ChartForm
              initialData={{
                ...chart,
                scheduledAt: chart.scheduledAt ?? undefined,
                drawTime: chart.drawTime ?? undefined,
              }}
              onSubmit={handleSubmit}
              onCancel={() => router.back()}
              isLoading={isSubmitting}
              availableYears={availableYears}
              availableMonths={availableMonths}
              availableDates={availableDates}
            />
            {error && (
              <div className="mt-4 p-4 bg-danger-50 border border-danger-200 rounded-lg text-danger-700" role="alert">
                {error}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
