FILE: src\app\admin\charts\page.tsx
============================================================

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { AdminSidebar } from "@/components/admin/AdminSidebar";
import { AdminHeader } from "@/components/admin/AdminHeader";
import { ChartTable } from "@/components/admin/ChartTable";
import { Button } from "@/components/shared/Button";
import { Input } from "@/components/shared/Input";
import { Select } from "@/components/shared/Select";
import { Loading } from "@/components/shared/Loading";
import { Plus, Search, X, RefreshCw, Upload } from "lucide-react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";
import { SPECIAL_MARKETS, dateKey, getDrawTime, istNow, marketKey, minutesOf, slugify } from "@/lib/markets";

/* ---------------------------------- types --------------------------------- */

interface ChartData {
  id: string;
  chartName: string;
  result: string;
  date: number;
  month: number;
  year: number;
  displayOrder: number;
  isHighlighted: boolean;
  isActive: boolean;
  formattedDate: string;
  drawTime?: string | null;
  createdAt: Date;
  updatedAt: Date;
  stale?: boolean;
}

interface Pagination {
  page: number;
  limit: number;
  total: number;
  totalPages: number;
}

type ViewMode = "today" | "special" | "tables" | "alldates";

/* --------------------------------- helpers -------------------------------- */

/** Fresh IST "today" strings (same clock as the action-order sort; correct across midnight). */
function todayParts() {
  const n = istNow();
  return {
    date: String(n.getDate()),
    month: String(n.getMonth() + 1),
    year: String(n.getFullYear()),
  };
}

const byName = (a: ChartData, b: ChartData) =>
  a.chartName.localeCompare(b.chartName, undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });

const byNewest = (a: ChartData, b: ChartData) =>
  dateKey(b.year, b.month, b.date) - dateKey(a.year, a.month, a.date);

async function readCharts(res: Response): Promise<ChartData[]> {
  if (!res.ok) return [];
  const d = await res.json();
  return d?.success ? (d.data.charts as ChartData[]) : [];
}

/** Client-side paginate a fully-built row list (used by today + special views). */
function paginate(rows: ChartData[], page: number, limit: number) {
  const total = rows.length;
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / limit));
  const safePage = Math.min(page, totalPages);
  return {
    slice: rows.slice((safePage - 1) * limit, safePage * limit),
    pagination: { page: safePage, limit, total, totalPages },
  };
}

const MONTH_OPTIONS = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => ({
  value: (i + 1).toString(),
  label: new Date(2000, i).toLocaleString("default", { month: "long" }),
}));

/* ---------------------------------- page ---------------------------------- */

export default function AdminChartsPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();

  // layout / auth
  const [isSidebarOpen, setIsSidebarOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isSidebarCollapsed, setIsSidebarCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [user, setUser] = useState<{ username: string; role: string } | null>(null);

  // data
  const [charts, setCharts] = useState<ChartData[]>([]);
  const [pagination, setPagination] = useState<Pagination>({ page: 1, limit: 20, total: 0, totalPages: 0 });
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [availableYears, setAvailableYears] = useState<number[]>([]);

  // view + filters (seeded from URL)
  const initialView: ViewMode =
    searchParams.get("view") === "tables" ? "tables"
      : searchParams.get("view") === "alldates" ? "alldates"
      : searchParams.get("view") === "special" ? "special"
      : "today";
  const [view, setView] = useState<ViewMode>(initialView);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState(searchParams.get("search") || "");
  const [dateFilter, setDateFilter] = useState(searchParams.get("date") || (initialView === "today" ? todayParts().date : ""));
  const [monthFilter, setMonthFilter] = useState(searchParams.get("month") || (initialView === "today" ? todayParts().month : ""));
  const [yearFilter, setYearFilter] = useState(searchParams.get("year") || (initialView === "today" ? todayParts().year : ""));
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState(searchParams.get("status") || "all");

  /* ------------------------------ auth + years ----------------------------- */
  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchAuth = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/admin/me");
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.success) setUser(data.data);
          else router.push("/admin/login");
        } else router.push("/admin/login");
      } catch {
        router.push("/admin/login");
      }
    };
    fetchAuth();
  }, [router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchYears = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/admin/charts/years");
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.success) setAvailableYears(data.data);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(err);
      }
    };
    fetchYears();
  }, []);

  /* -------------------------------- fetching ------------------------------- */
  const fetchCharts = async (page = 1) => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const base = new URLSearchParams({ page: "1", limit: "100", sortBy: "chartName", sortOrder: "asc" });
      if (search) base.set("search", search);
      if (statusFilter !== "all") base.set("isActive", statusFilter);

      if (view === "today") {
        // Tables that already have a row for the selected date…
        const updatedParams = new URLSearchParams(base);
        if (dateFilter) updatedParams.set("date", dateFilter);
        if (monthFilter) updatedParams.set("month", monthFilter);
        if (yearFilter) updatedParams.set("year", yearFilter);
        const updatedRows = await readCharts(await fetch(`/api/admin/charts?${updatedParams}`));

        // …plus tables that still need today's update (latest entry of each market).
        let staleRows: ChartData[] = [];
        if (statusFilter === "all") {
          const staleParams = new URLSearchParams(base);
          staleParams.set("latest", "1");
          const latestRows = await readCharts(await fetch(`/api/admin/charts?${staleParams}`));
          const updatedNames = new Set(updatedRows.map((r) => marketKey(r.chartName)));
          staleRows = latestRows
            .filter((r) => !updatedNames.has(marketKey(r.chartName)))
            .map((r) => ({ ...r, stale: true }));
        }

        // ACTION ORDER (3 tiers):
        //  1) needs action & draw time passed (pending / no entry today) → most overdue first
        //  2) upcoming draws → next one first
        //  3) already published → last
        const now = istNow();
        const nowMinutes = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
        const drawMins = (r: ChartData) => minutesOf(getDrawTime(slugify(r.chartName), r.drawTime));
        const needsAction = (r: ChartData) => Boolean(r.stale) || !(r.result || "").trim();
        const actionKey = (r: ChartData) => {
          const m = drawMins(r);
          const passed = nowMinutes >= m;
          if (passed && needsAction(r)) return -(nowMinutes - m);
          if (!passed) return m - nowMinutes;
          return 1_000_000 + (nowMinutes - m);
        };
        const combined = [...updatedRows, ...staleRows].sort(
          (a, b) => actionKey(a) - actionKey(b) || byName(a, b)
        );

        const p = paginate(combined, page, pagination.limit);
        setCharts(p.slice);
        setPagination(p.pagination);
      } else if (view === "special") {
        // Self-contained Special 4: fetch each special market by name, keep EXACT matches.
        // Works with the existing API (no `special=1` backend filter needed).
        const lists = await Promise.all(
          SPECIAL_MARKETS.map((name) => {
            const p = new URLSearchParams({ page: "1", limit: "100", sortBy: "chartName", sortOrder: "asc", search: name });
            if (dateFilter) p.set("date", dateFilter);
            if (monthFilter) p.set("month", monthFilter);
            if (yearFilter) p.set("year", yearFilter);
            if (statusFilter !== "all") p.set("isActive", statusFilter);
            return fetch(`/api/admin/charts?${p}`).then(readCharts);
          })
        );
        const wanted = new Set(SPECIAL_MARKETS.map((n) => marketKey(n)));
        let rows = lists.flat().filter((r) => wanted.has(marketKey(r.chartName)));
        if (search) rows = rows.filter((r) => r.chartName.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));
        rows.sort((a, b) => byName(a, b) || byNewest(a, b));

        const p = paginate(rows, page, pagination.limit);
        setCharts(p.slice);
        setPagination(p.pagination);
      } else {
        const params = new URLSearchParams({
          page: page.toString(),
          limit: pagination.limit.toString(),
          sortBy: "chartName",
          sortOrder: "asc",
        });
        if (view === "tables") params.set("latest", "1");
        if (search) params.set("search", search);
        if (dateFilter) params.set("date", dateFilter);
        if (monthFilter) params.set("month", monthFilter);
        if (yearFilter) params.set("year", yearFilter);
        if (statusFilter !== "all") params.set("isActive", statusFilter);

        const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/charts?${params}`);
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.success) {
            setCharts(data.data.charts);
            setPagination(data.data.pagination);
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchCharts(pagination.page);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [pagination.page, search, dateFilter, monthFilter, yearFilter, statusFilter, view]);

  // Re-sort every minute so the top always tracks the clock (today view only).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (view !== "today") return;
    const t = setInterval(() => fetchCharts(pagination.page), 60000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [view, pagination.page]);

  // On first load: merge case-duplicates, sync default markets, then refresh.
  useEffect(() => {
    const boot = async () => {
      try {
        await fetch("/api/admin/charts/deduplicate", { method: "POST" });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(err);
      }
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/admin/charts/ensure-defaults", { method: "POST" });
        if (res.ok) fetchCharts(1);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(err);
      }
    };
    boot();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  /* --------------------------------- actions -------------------------------- */
  const switchView = (v: ViewMode) => {
    const t = todayParts();
    setView(v);
    if (v === "today") {
      setDateFilter(t.date);
      setMonthFilter(t.month);
      setYearFilter(t.year);
    } else {
      setDateFilter("");
      setMonthFilter("");
      setYearFilter("");
    }
    setPagination((prev) => ({ ...prev, page: 1 }));
  };

  const handleSearch = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setPagination((prev) => ({ ...prev, page: 1 }));
    fetchCharts(1);
  };

  const clearFilters = () => {
    setSearch("");
    setStatusFilter("all");
    switchView("today");
  };

  const openForRow = (chart: ChartData) => {
    if (chart.stale) {
      const t = todayParts();
      const q = new URLSearchParams({
        market: chart.chartName,
        date: dateFilter || t.date,
        month: monthFilter || t.month,
        year: yearFilter || t.year,
      });
      router.push(`/admin/charts/add?${q.toString()}`);
    } else {
      router.push(`/admin/charts/edit/${chart.id}`);
    }
  };

  const syncDefaults = async () => {
    const res = await fetch("/api/admin/charts/ensure-defaults", { method: "POST" });
    if (res.ok) fetchCharts(pagination.page);
  };

  const hasFilters = Boolean(search || dateFilter || monthFilter || yearFilter || statusFilter !== "all");

  /* --------------------------------- render -------------------------------- */
  if (isLoading && charts.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="admin-layout">
        <AdminSidebar isCollapsed={isSidebarCollapsed} onToggle={() => setIsSidebarCollapsed(!isSidebarCollapsed)} user={user || undefined} />
        <div className="admin-main">
          <AdminHeader onMenuClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(true)} user={user!} pageTitle="Charts Management" breadcrumbs={[{ label: "Charts" }]} />
          <div className="admin-content">
            <Loading text="Loading charts..." />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="admin-layout">
      <div
        className={clsx("fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-black/50 lg:hidden transition-opacity", isSidebarOpen ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0 pointer-events-none")}
        onClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(false)}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />
      <AdminSidebar isCollapsed={isSidebarCollapsed} onToggle={() => setIsSidebarCollapsed(!isSidebarCollapsed)} user={user || undefined} />

      <div className="admin-main">
        <AdminHeader onMenuClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(true)} user={user!} pageTitle="Charts Management" breadcrumbs={[{ label: "Charts" }]} />

        <div className="admin-content">
          {/* header */}
          <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900">All Charts</h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                {view === "today" && statusFilter === "all" && (
                  <>Showing <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700">today's</span> tables in <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700">action order</span> — overdue / pending results first, then the next upcoming draws, published tables last. Click a row to edit / update it.</>
                )}
                {view === "today" && statusFilter === "true" && (
                  <>Showing <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700">Active Results</span> in action order (next draw first) — only charts updated for the current date.</>
                )}
                {view === "today" && statusFilter === "false" && (
                  <>Showing <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700">inactive</span> charts in action order for the current date.</>
                )}
                {view === "special" && (
                  <>Showing the <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700">4 special tables</span> (Gali, Desawar, Faridabad, Ghaziabad) — full history, newest first per table. Use <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700">Add Chart</span> to backfill past dates for these.</>
                )}
                {view === "tables" && (
                  <>Showing <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700">all tables</span> A → Z (latest entry of each market) — same count as Total Charts.</>
                )}
                {view === "alldates" && (
                  <>Showing <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700">every entry</span> A → Z (full history, all dates).</>
                )}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
              <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={syncDefaults}>
                <RefreshCw className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                Sync Default Markets
              </Button>
                 <Link href="/admin/charts/bulk-import" className="btn btn-outline">
                <Upload className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                Bulk Import
              </Link>
              <Link href="/admin/charts/add" className="btn btn-primary">
                <Plus className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                Add Chart
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* filters */}
          <div className="card mb-6">
            <form onSubmit={handleSearch} className="space-y-4 p-4">
              <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5">
                <Input label="Search" placeholder="Chart name..." value={search} onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)} />
                <Select
                  label="Year"
                  value={yearFilter}
                  onChange={(e) => setYearFilter(e.target.value)}
                  options={availableYears.map((y) => ({ value: y.toString(), label: y.toString() }))}
                  placeholder="All Years"
                />
                <Select label="Month" value={monthFilter} onChange={(e) => setMonthFilter(e.target.value)} options={MONTH_OPTIONS} placeholder="All Months" />
                <Input label="Date" type="number" min={1} max={31} placeholder="DD" value={dateFilter} onChange={(e) => setDateFilter(e.target.value)} />
                <Select
                  label="Status"
                  value={statusFilter}
                  onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}
                  options={[
                    { value: "all", label: "All" },
                    { value: "true", label: "Active" },
                    { value: "false", label: "Inactive" },
                  ]}
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-t border-gray-200 pt-2">
                <Button type="submit" variant="primary">
                  <Search className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                  Search
                </Button>
                <Button type="button" variant={view === "today" ? "secondary" : "outline"} onClick={() => switchView("today")}>
                  Today
                </Button>
                <Button type="button" variant={view === "special" ? "secondary" : "outline"} onClick={() => switchView("special")}>
                  Special 4
                </Button>
                <Button type="button" variant={view === "tables" ? "secondary" : "outline"} onClick={() => switchView("tables")}>
                  All Tables
                </Button>
                <Button type="button" variant={view === "alldates" ? "secondary" : "outline"} onClick={() => switchView("alldates")}>
                  All Dates
                </Button>
                {hasFilters && (
                  <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={clearFilters}>
                    <X className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                    Clear Filters
                  </Button>
                )}
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>

          {/* table */}
          <ChartTable
            charts={charts}
            isLoading={isLoading}
            pagination={pagination}
            onPageChange={fetchCharts}
            onRowClick={openForRow}
            onEdit={openForRow}
            onDelete={async (chart) => {
              const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/charts/${chart.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
              if (res.ok) fetchCharts(pagination.page);
            }}
            onToggleHighlight={async (chart) => {
              const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/charts/${chart.id}/highlight`, { method: "POST" });
              if (res.ok) fetchCharts(pagination.page);
            }}
            onToggleActive={async (chart) => {
              const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/charts/${chart.id}/active`, { method: "POST" });
              if (res.ok) fetchCharts(pagination.page);
            }}
            onView={(chart) => alert(`View: ${chart.chartName} - ${chart.result} (${chart.formattedDate})`)}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { AdminSidebar } from "@/components/admin/AdminSidebar";
import { AdminHeader } from "@/components/admin/AdminHeader";
import { DashboardStats } from "@/components/admin/DashboardStats";
import { Loading } from "@/components/shared/Loading";
import { Menu } from "lucide-react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

interface DashboardData {
  totalCharts: number;
  totalResults: number;
  todaysEntries: number;
  latestCharts: Array<{
    id: string;
    chartName: string;
    result: string;
    date: number;
    month: number;
    year: number;
    isActive: boolean;
    createdAt: Date;
    updatedAt: Date;
    scheduledAt?: string;
  }>;
}

export default function AdminDashboardPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isSidebarOpen, setIsSidebarOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isSidebarCollapsed, setIsSidebarCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [user, setUser] = useState<{ username: string; role: string } | null>(null);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<DashboardData | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchAuth = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/admin/me");
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.success) {
            setUser(data.data);
          } else {
            router.push("/admin/login");
          }
        } else {
          router.push("/admin/login");
        }
      } catch {
        router.push("/admin/login");
      }
    };

    const fetchStats = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/admin/stats");
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.success) {
            setStats(data.data);
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(err);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    };

     const boot = async () => {
      // Create today's default-market tables first so counts & list include them
      try {
        await fetch("/api/admin/charts/ensure-defaults", { method: "POST" });
      } catch {}
      await fetchStats();
    };
    fetchAuth();
    boot();
  }, [router]);

  if (isLoading || !user || !stats) {
    return (
      <div className="admin-layout">
        <AdminSidebar isCollapsed={isSidebarCollapsed} onToggle={() => setIsSidebarCollapsed(!isSidebarCollapsed)} user={user || undefined} />
        <div className="admin-main">
          <AdminHeader
            onMenuClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(true)}
            user={user!}
            pageTitle="Dashboard"
            breadcrumbs={[]}
          />
          <div className="admin-content">
            <Loading text="Loading dashboard..." />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="admin-layout">
      <div
        className={clsx("fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-black/50 lg:hidden transition-opacity", isSidebarOpen ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0 pointer-events-none")}
        onClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(false)}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />
      <AdminSidebar
        isCollapsed={isSidebarCollapsed}
        onToggle={() => setIsSidebarCollapsed(!isSidebarCollapsed)}
        user={user || undefined}
      />
      <div className="admin-main">
        <AdminHeader
          onMenuClick={() => setIsSidebarOpen(true)}
          user={user}
          pageTitle="Dashboard"
          breadcrumbs={[]}
        />
        <div className="admin-content">
          <DashboardStats stats={stats} latestCharts={stats.latestCharts} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\app\admin\login\page.tsx

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Button } from "@/components/shared/Button";
import { Input } from "@/components/shared/Input";
import { Loading } from "@/components/shared/Loading";
import { AlertCircle, Eye, EyeOff, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

export default function AdminLoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [formErrors, setFormErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  const validateForm = () => {
    const errors: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (!username.trim()) errors.username = "Username or email is required";
    if (!password) errors.password = "Password is required";
    else if (password.length < 6) errors.password = "Password must be at least 6 characters";
    setFormErrors(errors);
    return Object.keys(errors).length === 0;
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError("");

    if (!validateForm()) return;

    setIsLoading(true);

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/admin/login", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ username, password }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();

      if (data.success) {
        router.push(data.data?.redirect || "/admin/dashboard");
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        setError(data.message || "Login failed");
        if (data.errors) {
          setFormErrors(data.errors);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setError("An error occurred. Please try again.");
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md">
        <div className="text-center mb-8">
          <Link href="/" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 mb-6" aria-label="Dhan Kuber Home">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-xl bg-primary-600 flex items-center justify-center">
              <span className="text-white font-bold text-2xl">OK</span>
            </div>
          </Link>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900">Admin Panel</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">Sign in to manage charts and results</p>
        </div>

        <div className="card p-8">
          {error && (
            <div className="mb-6 p-4 bg-danger-50 border border-danger-200 rounded-lg flex items-start gap-3 text-danger-700" role="alert">
              <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span className="text-sm">{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5" noValidate>
            <Input
              label="Username or Email"
              id="username"
              type="text"
              value={username}
              onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
              error={formErrors.username}
              placeholder="Enter username or email"
              autoComplete="username"
              disabled={isLoading}
            />

            <div className="relative">
              <label htmlFor="password" className="label">
                Password
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <input
                  type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                  id="password"
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  className={clsx(
                    "input pr-12",
                    formErrors.password && "border-danger-500 focus:border-danger-500 focus:ring-danger-500/20"
                  )}
                  placeholder="Enter password"
                  autoComplete="current-password"
                  disabled={isLoading}
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                  className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600"
                  aria-label={showPassword ? "Hide password" : "Show password"}
                >
                  {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Eye className="w-5 h-5" />}
                </button>
              </div>
              {formErrors.password && (
                <p className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-danger-600" role="alert">{formErrors.password}</p>
              )}
            </div>

            <Button type="submit" variant="primary" className="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white hover:bg-blue-700" loading={isLoading}>
              {isLoading ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> : "Sign In"}
            </Button>
          </form>

          <div className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">
            <Link href="/" className="text-primary-600 hover:text-primary-700 font-medium">
              ← Back to Website
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 text-center text-xs text-gray-400">
          <p>Default credentials: admin / admin123</p>
          <p>Change password after first login</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// src/app/admin/settings/page.tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { AdminSidebar } from "@/components/admin/AdminSidebar";
import { AdminHeader } from "@/components/admin/AdminHeader";
import { Button } from "@/components/shared/Button";
import { Input } from "@/components/shared/Input";
import { Loading } from "@/components/shared/Loading";
import { clsx } from "clsx";
import { Eye, EyeOff, Lock } from "lucide-react";

export default function AdminSettingsPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [user, setUser] = useState<{ username: string; role: string } | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  
  const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState("");
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [showPasswords, setShowPasswords] = useState(false);
  
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<{ type: "success" | "error"; text: string } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchAuth = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/admin/me");
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.success) setUser(data.data);
          else router.push("/admin/login");
        } else router.push("/admin/login");
      } catch { router.push("/admin/login"); }
      finally { setIsLoading(false); }
    };
    fetchAuth();
  }, [router]);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setMessage(null);

    if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
      setMessage({ type: "error", text: "New passwords do not match." });
      return;
    }
    if (newPassword.length < 6) {
      setMessage({ type: "error", text: "New password must be at least 6 characters." });
      return;
    }

    setIsSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/admin/me", {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ currentPassword, newPassword }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      
      if (data.success) {
        setMessage({ type: "success", text: "Password changed successfully!" });
        setCurrentPassword("");
        setNewPassword("");
        setConfirmPassword("");
      } else {
        setMessage({ type: "error", text: data.message || "Failed to update password." });
      }
    } catch {
      setMessage({ type: "error", text: "An unexpected error occurred." });
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (isLoading || !user) {
    return (
      <div className="admin-layout">
        <AdminSidebar isCollapsed={false} user={user || undefined} />
        <div className="admin-main">
          <AdminHeader user={user!} pageTitle="Settings" breadcrumbs={[{ label: "Settings" }]} />
          <div className="admin-content"><Loading text="Loading..." /></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="admin-layout">
      <AdminSidebar isCollapsed={false} user={user} />
      <div className="admin-main">
        <AdminHeader user={user} pageTitle="Settings" breadcrumbs={[{ label: "Settings" }]} />
        <div className="admin-content max-w-2xl mx-auto">
          <div className="card p-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
              <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-primary-100">
                <Lock className="w-6 h-6 text-primary-600" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900">Change Password</h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Update your admin account password</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {message && (
              <div className={clsx(
                "mb-6 p-4 rounded-lg border flex items-center gap-2",
                message.type === "success" ? "bg-success-50 border-success-200 text-success-700" : "bg-danger-50 border-danger-200 text-danger-700"
              )}>
                {message.text}
              </div>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5">
              <div className="relative">
                <Input
                  label="Current Password"
                  type={showPasswords ? "text" : "password"}
                  value={currentPassword}
                  onChange={(e) => setCurrentPassword(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  disabled={isSubmitting}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="relative">
                <Input
                  label="New Password"
                  type={showPasswords ? "text" : "password"}
                  value={newPassword}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  disabled={isSubmitting}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="relative">
                <Input
                  label="Confirm New Password"
                  type={showPasswords ? "text" : "password"}
                  value={confirmPassword}
                  onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  disabled={isSubmitting}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2">
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer text-sm text-gray-600">
                  <input 
                    type="checkbox" 
                    checked={showPasswords} 
                    onChange={(e) => setShowPasswords(e.target.checked)}
                    className="rounded border-gray-300"
                  />
                  Show passwords
                </label>
                
                <Button type="submit" variant="primary" loading={isSubmitting}>
                  Update Password
                </Button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\app\api\admin\admins\route.ts
============================================================

// src/app/api/admin/admins/route.ts
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Admin from "@/models/Admin";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;
    if (!token) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Not authenticated" }, { status: 401 });

    const payload = await verifyToken(token);
    if (!payload || !["super_admin", "admin"].includes(payload.role)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 403 });
    }

    await connectDB();
    // -passwordHash ensures the encrypted password is NEVER sent to the frontend
    const admins = await Admin.find().select("-passwordHash").sort({ createdAt: -1 }).lean();

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      data: admins.map((a) => ({
        id: a._id.toString(),
        username: a.username,
        email: a.email,
        role: a.role,
        isActive: a.isActive,
        lastLoginAt: a.lastLoginAt,
        createdAt: a.createdAt,
      })),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching admins:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Failed to fetch admins" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FILE: src\app\api\admin\charts\bulk-import\route.ts
============================================================

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
export const maxDuration = 60;

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";
import { getDefaultDrawTime } from "@/lib/markets";

/* DSWR / FRBD / GZBD / GALI → internal slugs (full names also accepted). */
const COLUMN_MAP: Record<string, string> = {
  DSWR: "desawar", DESAWAR: "desawar", DESAWER: "desawar",
  FRBD: "faridabad", FARIDABAD: "faridabad",
  GZBD: "ghaziabad", GHAZIABAD: "ghaziabad", GAZIABAD: "ghaziabad",
  GALI: "gali",
};
const NAMES: Record<string, string> = {
  desawar: "Desawar", faridabad: "Faridabad", ghaziabad: "Ghaziabad", gali: "Gali",
};
const MONTHS = ["january","february","march","april","may","june","july","august","september","october","november","december"];

interface ParsedRow { year: number; month: number; day: number; values: Record<string, string>; }

/** Parses one OR many month blocks pasted together. */
function parseChartText(text: string, fallback?: { month: number; year: number }) {
  const rows: ParsedRow[] = [];
  const notes: string[] = [];
  let cur: { month: number; year: number } | null = fallback ?? null;
  let cols: (string | null)[] | null = null;

  for (const raw of text.split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const line = raw.trim();
    if (!line) continue;

    // "… Chart of November 2026 for …"  → starts a new month block
    const hm = line.match(/(january|february|march|april|may|june|july|august|september|october|november|december)\s+(\d{4})/i);
    if (hm) {
      cur = { month: MONTHS.indexOf(hm[1].toLowerCase()) + 1, year: parseInt(hm[2], 10) };
      cols = null;
      continue;
    }

    const tokens = line.split(/\t+|\s+/).filter(Boolean);

    // "DATE  DSWR  FRBD  GZBD  GALI"
    if (/^date$/i.test(tokens[0])) {
      cols = tokens.slice(1).map((t) => COLUMN_MAP[t.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z]/g, "")] ?? null);
      continue;
    }

    // data row must start with a day number
    if (!/^\d{1,2}$/.test(tokens[0])) continue;
    if (!cur || !cols) {
      notes.push(`Skipped "${line.slice(0, 40)}" — no month/year header above it`);
      continue;
    }
    const day = parseInt(tokens[0], 10);
    if (day < 1 || day > 31) continue;

    const values: Record<string, string> = {};
    cols.forEach((slug, i) => {
      const v = tokens[i + 1];
      if (!slug || !v) return;           // missing trailing cells are simply absent
      values[slug] = v.toUpperCase();     // keeps "02", "XX", "--" exactly as pasted
    });
    if (Object.keys(values).length) rows.push({ year: cur.year, month: cur.month, day, values });
  }
  return { rows, notes };
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;
    if (!token) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Not authenticated" }, { status: 401 });
    const payload = await verifyToken(token);
    if (!payload || !["super_admin", "admin", "editor"].includes(payload.role)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const body = await request.json();
    const text: string = body?.text || "";
    const dryRun: boolean = Boolean(body?.dryRun);
    const fallback = body?.month && body?.year
      ? { month: Number(body.month), year: Number(body.year) }
      : undefined;

    if (!text.trim()) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Paste the chart text first" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const { rows, notes } = parseChartText(text, fallback);
    if (rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({
        success: false,
        message: "No rows recognised. Include a '… November 2026 …' line and a 'DATE DSWR FRBD GZBD GALI' header.",
        notes,
      }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // Build upsert operations — one per (market, day)
    const ops = rows.flatMap((r) =>
      Object.entries(r.values).map(([slug, value]) => ({
        updateOne: {
          filter: { chartName: NAMES[slug], date: r.day, month: r.month, year: r.year },
          update: {
            $set: { result: value, isActive: true, drawTime: getDefaultDrawTime(slug) },
            $setOnInsert: {
              chartName: NAMES[slug], date: r.day, month: r.month, year: r.year,
              displayOrder: 0, isHighlighted: false,
            },
          },
          upsert: true,
        },
      }))
    );

    const months = Array.from(new Set(rows.map((r) => `${r.year}-${String(r.month).padStart(2, "0")}`))).sort();

    if (dryRun) {
      return NextResponse.json({
        success: true,
        message: `Preview: ${ops.length} cells across ${rows.length} days / ${months.length} month(s)`,
        data: { dryRun: true, cells: ops.length, days: rows.length, months, notes, sample: rows.slice(0, 5) },
      });
    }

    if (ops.length > 20000) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Too many cells in one go — paste fewer months at a time (max ~20,000)." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await connectDB();
    const res = await Chart.bulkWrite(ops, { ordered: false });

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: `Imported ${ops.length} cells — ${res.upsertedCount} new, ${res.modifiedCount} updated.`,
      data: {
        cells: ops.length,
        created: res.upsertedCount,
        updated: res.modifiedCount,
        days: rows.length,
        months,
        notes,
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Bulk import error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Import failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;

    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Not authenticated" },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    const payload = await verifyToken(token);
    if (!payload) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Invalid session" },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    await connectDB();

    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
    const year = searchParams.get("year");
    const month = searchParams.get("month");

    if (!year || !month) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Year and month parameters are required" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const dates = await Chart.distinct("date", {
      year: parseInt(year),
      month: parseInt(month),
    }).sort();

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      data: dates,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching dates:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: "Failed to fetch dates" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

FILE: src\app\api\admin\charts\deduplicate\route.ts
============================================================

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";
import { canonicalMarketName, marketKey } from "@/lib/markets";

/**
 * Merges case-variant duplicates (Sadabahar / sadabahar → one table).
 * For each (market, date) keeps the MOST RECENTLY UPDATED entry, deletes the rest,
 * then normalizes every remaining chartName to canonical Title Case.
 */
export async function POST() {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;
    if (!token) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Not authenticated" }, { status: 401 });
    const payload = await verifyToken(token);
    if (!payload || !["super_admin", "admin"].includes(payload.role)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 403 });
    }

    await connectDB();

    const all = await Chart.find({}).sort({ updatedAt: -1 }).lean();
    const kept = new Map<string, (typeof all)[number]>();
    const toDelete: string[] = [];

    for (const c of all) {
      const key = `${marketKey(c.chartName)}|${c.date}|${c.month}|${c.year}`;
      if (!kept.has(key)) kept.set(key, c);
      else toDelete.push(c._id.toString());
    }

    if (toDelete.length > 0) {
      await Chart.deleteMany({ _id: { $in: toDelete } });
    }

    let renamed = 0;
    for (const c of Array.from(kept.values())) {
      const canon = canonicalMarketName(c.chartName);
      if (canon && canon !== c.chartName) {
        await Chart.updateOne({ _id: c._id }, { $set: { chartName: canon } });
        renamed++;
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: `Merged duplicates: ${toDelete.length} deleted, ${renamed} renamed. ${kept.size} unique tables remain.`,
      data: { deleted: toDelete.length, renamed, tables: kept.size },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Deduplicate error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Failed to merge duplicates" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: src\app\api\admin\charts\ensure-defaults\route.ts
============================================================

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";
import {
  DEFAULT_MARKETS, SPECIAL_MARKETS, addDays, autoResult, escapeRegExp, getDefaultDrawTime, istNow, istToday, slugify,
} from "@/lib/markets";

/**
 * Creates DB entries for the 4 default markets (Gali, Desawar, Faridabad, Ghaziabad)
 * for yesterday + today when they don't exist yet, using the exact same auto values,
 * draw times and highlight the public site shows by default.
 * Idempotent — running it again never duplicates (unique index also protects).
 */
export async function POST() {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;
    if (!token) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Not authenticated" }, { status: 401 });

    const payload = await verifyToken(token);
    if (!payload || !["super_admin", "admin", "editor"].includes(payload.role)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 403 });
    }

    await connectDB();
    const today = istToday(istNow());
    const yesterday = addDays(today, -1);
    let created = 0;

    for (const day of [yesterday, today]) {
          for (const name of [...DEFAULT_MARKETS, ...SPECIAL_MARKETS]) {
        const slug = slugify(name);
               const existing = await Chart.findOne({
          chartName: { $regex: `^${escapeRegExp(name)}$`, $options: "i" },
          date: day.day,
          month: day.month,
          year: day.year,
        });
        if (existing) {
          // Migrate rows that were seeded with the old 12:00 fallback to the real schedule
           const def = getDefaultDrawTime(slug);
          // Only fill when no time was ever set — an admin choice (even 12:00) is sacred.
          if (!existing.drawTime && def) {
            existing.drawTime = def;
            await existing.save();
          }
          continue;
        }

        await Chart.create({
          chartName: name,
          result: autoResult(slug, day.day, day.month, day.year),
          date: day.day,
          month: day.month,
          year: day.year,
          displayOrder: 0,
          isHighlighted: false, // highlight is decided only by the admin
          isActive: true,
          drawTime: getDefaultDrawTime(slug),
        });
        created++;
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: created > 0 ? `Synced default markets (${created} new entries)` : "Default markets already synced",
      data: { created },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Ensure defaults error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Failed to sync default markets" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Not authenticated" },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    const payload = await verifyToken(token);
    if (!payload) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Invalid session" },
        { status: 401 }

    }

    await connectDB();

    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);

FILE: src\app\api\admin\charts\rename\route.ts
============================================================

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;
    if (!token) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    const payload = await verifyToken(token);
    if (!payload || !["super_admin", "admin", "editor"].includes(payload.role)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Forbidden" }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const { oldName, newName } = await request.json();
    if (!oldName || !newName || oldName.trim() === "" || newName.trim() === "") {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Both old and new names are required" }, { status: 400 });
    }
    if (oldName === newName) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "New name must be different" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await connectDB();
    
    // Check for conflicts (unique constraint on chartName + date + month + year)
    const oldEntries = await Chart.find({ chartName: oldName }).select("date month year").lean();
    for (const entry of oldEntries) {
      const conflict = await Chart.findOne({
        chartName: newName,
        date: entry.date,
        month: entry.month,
        year: entry.year,
      });
      if (conflict) {
        return NextResponse.json({
          success: false,
          message: `Cannot rename: An entry for "${newName}" already exists on ${entry.date}/${entry.month}/${entry.year}. Delete the conflicting entry first.`
        }, { status: 409 });
      }
    }

    const result = await Chart.updateMany(
      { chartName: oldName },
      { $set: { chartName: newName } }
    );

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: `Successfully renamed ${result.modifiedCount} entries from "${oldName}" to "${newName}"`,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Rename error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Failed to rename market" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FILE: src\app\api\admin\charts\route.ts
============================================================

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import mongoose from "mongoose";
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";
import { chartFilterSchema, chartSchema } from "@/lib/validations";
import { canonicalMarketName, escapeRegExp, marketKey, SPECIAL_MARKETS } from "@/lib/markets";

function formatChartDate(date: number, month: number, year: number): string {
  return `${String(date).padStart(2, "0")}/${String(month).padStart(2, "0")}/${year}`;
}

async function checkAuth() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;
  if (!token) return null;
  const payload = await verifyToken(token);
  if (!payload || !["super_admin", "admin", "editor"].includes(payload.role)) return null;
  return payload;
}

const byName = (a: { chartName: string }, b: { chartName: string }) =>
  a.chartName.localeCompare(b.chartName, undefined, { sensitivity: "base" });

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const payload = await checkAuth();
    if (!payload) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

    await connectDB();

    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
    const validation = chartFilterSchema.safeParse(Object.fromEntries(searchParams));
    if (!validation.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Invalid filters", errors: validation.error.flatten().fieldErrors },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const { search, date, month, year, isActive, page, limit, sortBy, sortOrder } = validation.data;

    const query: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    if (search) query.chartName = { $regex: escapeRegExp(search), $options: "i" };
    if (date) query.date = date;
    if (month) query.month = month;
    if (year) query.year = year;
    if (isActive && isActive !== "all") query.isActive = isActive === "true";

    // Special 4 view: only the permanent special tables (any date).
    if (searchParams.get("special") === "1") {
      query.$or = SPECIAL_MARKETS.map((n) => ({
        chartName: { $regex: `^${escapeRegExp(n)}$`, $options: "i" },
      }));
    }
        if (searchParams.get("special") === "1") {
      query.$or = SPECIAL_MARKETS.map((n) => ({
        chartName: { $regex: `^${escapeRegExp(n)}$`, $options: "i" },
      }));
    }

    const latestOnly = searchParams.get("latest") === "1";

    type LeanChart = {
      _id: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
      chartName: string;
      result: string;
      openingResult?: string;
      closingResult?: string;
      date: number;
      month: number;
      year: number;
      displayOrder: number;
      isHighlighted: boolean;
      isActive: boolean;
      scheduledAt?: unknown;
      drawTime?: string | null;
      createdAt: Date;
      updatedAt: Date;
    };

    let charts: LeanChart[] = [];
    let total = 0;

    if (latestOnly) {
      // ONE row per market (case-insensitive) = its latest entry, listed A → Z.
      const all = (await Chart.find(query)
        .sort({ year: -1, month: -1, date: -1, updatedAt: -1 })
        .lean()) as unknown as LeanChart[];
      const seen = new Set<string>();
      const grouped: LeanChart[] = [];
      for (const c of all) {
        const key = marketKey(c.chartName);
        if (!seen.has(key)) {
          seen.add(key);
          grouped.push(c);
        }
      }
      grouped.sort(byName);
      total = grouped.length;
      charts = grouped.slice((page - 1) * limit, page * limit);
    } else {
      const dir: 1 | -1 = sortOrder === "asc" ? 1 : -1;
      const sort: Record<string, 1 | -1> =
        sortBy === "chartName"
          ? { chartName: dir, year: -1, month: -1, date: -1 }
          : { [sortBy]: dir, chartName: 1 };
      const [c, t] = await Promise.all([
        Chart.find(query).sort(sort).skip((page - 1) * limit).limit(limit).lean(),
        Chart.countDocuments(query),
      ]);
      charts = c as unknown as LeanChart[];
      total = t;
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      data: {
        charts: charts.map((c) => ({
          id: c._id.toString(),
          chartName: c.chartName,
          result: c.result,
          date: c.date,
          month: c.month,
          year: c.year,
          displayOrder: c.displayOrder,
          isHighlighted: c.isHighlighted,
          isActive: c.isActive,
          scheduledAt: c.scheduledAt,
          drawTime: c.drawTime,
          formattedDate: formatChartDate(c.date, c.month, c.year),
          createdAt: c.createdAt,
          updatedAt: c.updatedAt,
        })),
        pagination: { page, limit, total, totalPages: Math.ceil(total / limit) },
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching charts:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Failed to fetch charts" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const payload = await checkAuth();
    if (!payload) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });

    const body = await request.json();
    const validation = chartSchema.safeParse(body);
    if (!validation.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Invalid input", errors: validation.error.flatten().fieldErrors },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await connectDB();

    // Canonical casing + case-insensitive duplicate guard
    const data = { ...validation.data, chartName: canonicalMarketName(validation.data.chartName) };
    const existing = await Chart.findOne({
      chartName: { $regex: `^${escapeRegExp(data.chartName)}$`, $options: "i" },
      date: data.date,
      month: data.month,
      year: data.year,
    });
    if (existing) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "A chart with this name and date already exists (case-insensitive)" },
        { status: 409 }
      );
    }

    const chart = await Chart.create(data);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: "Chart created successfully",
      data: { id: (chart._id as mongoose.Types.ObjectId).toString(), ...chart.toObject() },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error creating chart:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Failed to create chart" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;

    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Not authenticated" },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    const payload = await verifyToken(token);
    if (!payload) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Invalid session" },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    await connectDB();

    const years = (await Chart.distinct("year")).filter((y: number) => y >= 2015);
    const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
    // Generate years from 2012 to current year only
    const allYears = Array.from({ length: currentYear - 2012 + 1 }, (_, i) => 2012 + i);
    const combinedYears = Array.from(new Set([...years, ...allYears])).sort((a, b) => b - a);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      data: combinedYears,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching years:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: "Failed to fetch years" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

FILE: src\app\api\admin\charts\[id]\active\route.ts

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";

async function checkAuth() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;

  if (!token) return null;

  const payload = await verifyToken(token);
  if (!payload || !["super_admin", "admin", "editor"].includes(payload.role)) {
    return null;
  }

  return payload;
}

export async function POST(
  request: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const payload = await checkAuth();
    if (!payload) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Unauthorized" },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    await connectDB();

    const { id } = await params;
    const chart = await Chart.findById(id);

    if (!chart) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Chart not found" },
        { status: 404 }
      );
    }

    chart.isActive = !chart.isActive;
    await chart.save();

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: `Chart ${chart.isActive ? "activated" : "deactivated"}`,
      data: { isActive: chart.isActive },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error toggling active:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: "Failed to toggle active status" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

FILE: src\app\api\admin\charts\[id]\highlight\route.ts

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";

async function checkAuth() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;

  if (!token) return null;

  const payload = await verifyToken(token);
  if (!payload || !["super_admin", "admin", "editor"].includes(payload.role)) {
    return null;
  }

  return payload;
}

export async function POST(
  request: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const payload = await checkAuth();
    if (!payload) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Unauthorized" },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    await connectDB();

    const { id } = await params;
    const chart = await Chart.findById(id);

    if (!chart) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Chart not found" },
        { status: 404 }
      );
    }

    chart.isHighlighted = !chart.isHighlighted;
    await chart.save();

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: `Chart ${chart.isHighlighted ? "highlighted" : "unhighlighted"}`,
      data: { isHighlighted: chart.isHighlighted },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error toggling highlight:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: "Failed to toggle highlight" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

FILE: src\app\api\admin\charts\[id]\route.ts

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";
import { chartUpdateSchema } from "@/lib/validations";
import { canonicalMarketName, escapeRegExp } from "@/lib/markets";

/** .lean() queries skip Mongoose virtuals → build formattedDate manually. */
function formatChartDate(date: number, month: number, year: number): string {
  return `${String(date).padStart(2, "0")}/${String(month).padStart(2, "0")}/${year}`;
}

async function checkAuth() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;
  if (!token) return null;

  const payload = await verifyToken(token);
  if (!payload || !["super_admin", "admin", "editor"].includes(payload.role)) return null;

  return payload;
}

export async function GET(
  request: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const payload = await checkAuth();
    if (!payload) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    await connectDB();

    const { id } = await params;
    const chart = await Chart.findById(id).lean();
    if (!chart) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Chart not found" }, { status: 404 });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      data: {
        id: chart._id.toString(),
        chartName: chart.chartName,
        result: chart.result,
        openingResult: chart.openingResult,
        closingResult: chart.closingResult,
        date: chart.date,
        month: chart.month,
        year: chart.year,
        displayOrder: chart.displayOrder,
        isHighlighted: chart.isHighlighted,
        isActive: chart.isActive,
        scheduledAt: chart.scheduledAt,
        drawTime: chart.drawTime,
        formattedDate: formatChartDate(chart.date, chart.month, chart.year),
        createdAt: chart.createdAt,
        updatedAt: chart.updatedAt,
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching chart:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: "Failed to fetch chart" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

export async function PUT(
  request: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const payload = await checkAuth();
    if (!payload) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const body = await request.json();
    const validation = chartUpdateSchema.safeParse(body);
    if (!validation.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Invalid input", errors: validation.error.flatten().fieldErrors },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await connectDB();

    const { id } = await params;
    const chart = await Chart.findById(id);
    if (!chart) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Chart not found" }, { status: 404 });
    }

    // Duplicate check only when name/date fields are being changed
        if (validation.data.chartName) {
      validation.data.chartName = canonicalMarketName(validation.data.chartName);
    }

    if (validation.data.chartName || validation.data.date || validation.data.month || validation.data.year) {
      const checkName = validation.data.chartName || chart.chartName;
      const checkDate = validation.data.date || chart.date;
      const checkMonth = validation.data.month || chart.month;
      const checkYear = validation.data.year || chart.year;

      const existing = await Chart.findOne({
        _id: { $ne: id },
        chartName: { $regex: `^${escapeRegExp(checkName)}$`, $options: "i" },
        date: checkDate,
        month: checkMonth,
        year: checkYear,
      });
      if (existing) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          { success: false, message: "A chart with this name and date already exists (case-insensitive)" },
          { status: 409 }
        );
      }
    }

       // Updating a result always publishes the table → it falls under Active Results
    if (validation.data.result && validation.data.result !== chart.result) {
      chart.isActive = true;
    }
    Object.assign(chart, validation.data); // includes drawTime
    await chart.save();
    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: "Chart updated successfully",
      data: chart.toObject(),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error updating chart:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: "Failed to update chart" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

export async function DELETE(
  request: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const payload = await checkAuth();
    // Deleting is restricted to super_admin / admin (editors cannot delete)
    if (!payload || !["super_admin", "admin"].includes(payload.role)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    await connectDB();

    const { id } = await params;
    const chart = await Chart.findByIdAndDelete(id);
    if (!chart) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Chart not found" }, { status: 404 });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: "Chart deleted successfully",
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error deleting chart:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: "Failed to delete chart" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

FILE: src\app\api\admin\db-info\route.ts

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";
import { SPECIAL_MARKETS, istNow, istToday } from "@/lib/markets";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const token = (await cookies()).get("admin_session")?.value;
    if (!token) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Not authenticated" }, { status: 401 });
    const payload = await verifyToken(token);
    if (!payload) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 403 });

    await connectDB();
    const t = istToday(istNow());

    const [total, monthTotal, special4] = await Promise.all([
      Chart.countDocuments(),
      Chart.countDocuments({ year: t.year, month: t.month }),
      Chart.countDocuments({ year: t.year, month: t.month, chartName: { $in: SPECIAL_MARKETS } }),
    ]);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      data: {
        database: mongoose.connection.name,
        host: mongoose.connection.host,
        environment: process.env.NODE_ENV,
        totalCharts: total,
        chartsThisMonth: monthTotal,
        special4ThisMonth: special4,
        today: `${t.year}-${String(t.month).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(t.day).padStart(2, "0")}`,
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("db-info error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Failed to read database info" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: src\app\api\admin\login\route.ts
============================================================

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Admin from "@/models/Admin";
import { verifyPassword, createToken } from "@/lib/auth";
import { loginSchema } from "@/lib/validations";

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();
    const validation = loginSchema.safeParse(body);
    if (!validation.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Invalid input", errors: validation.error.flatten().fieldErrors },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await connectDB();

    const username = validation.data.username;
    const password = validation.data.password;

    // Credentials from .env.local — the single source of truth for the owner account
    const envUser = process.env.ADMIN_USERNAME || "admin";
    const envPass = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "admin123";

    let admin = await Admin.findOne({
      $or: [{ username }, { email: username }],
    }).select("+passwordHash");

    // ENV FALLBACK + SELF-HEAL:
    // If the entered credentials match .env.local, make sure the DB account exists
    // and its hash is correct (fixes broken / double-hashed passwords automatically).
    if (username === envUser && password === envPass) {
      if (!admin) {
        // Create the owner account (model pre-save hook hashes the plain password)
        admin = await Admin.create({
          username: envUser,
          email: process.env.ADMIN_EMAIL || `${envUser}@localhost`,
          passwordHash: envPass,
          role: "super_admin",
          isActive: true,
        });
      } else if (!(await verifyPassword(envPass, admin.passwordHash))) {
        // Stored hash is broken/double-hashed → repair it from the env password
        admin.passwordHash = envPass; // pre-save hook re-hashes once → correct hash
        admin.isActive = true;
        admin.loginAttempts = 0;
        admin.lockUntil = undefined;
        await admin.save();
      }
    }

    if (!admin) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Invalid username or password" }, { status: 401 });
    }
    if (!admin.isActive) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Account is deactivated. Contact administrator." }, { status: 401 });
    }
    if (admin.isLocked) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Account temporarily locked due to too many failed attempts. Try again in 15 minutes." },
        { status: 429 }
      );
    }

    const isValid = await verifyPassword(password, admin.passwordHash);
    if (!isValid) {
      await admin.incrementLoginAttempts();
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Invalid username or password" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    await admin.resetLoginAttempts();
    admin.lastLoginAt = new Date();
    await admin.save();

    const token = await createToken({
      id: (admin._id as mongoose.Types.ObjectId).toString(),
      username: admin.username,
      email: admin.email,
      role: admin.role,
    });

    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    cookieStore.set("admin_session", token, {
      httpOnly: true,
      secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
      sameSite: "lax",
      maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24,
      path: "/",
    });

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: "Login successful",
      data: { redirect: "/admin/dashboard" },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Login error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "An error occurred during login" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

// src/app/api/admin/me/route.ts
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Admin from "@/models/Admin";
import { verifyToken, hashPassword, verifyPassword } from "@/lib/auth";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;
    if (!token) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Not authenticated" }, { status: 401 });
    
    const payload = await verifyToken(token);
    if (!payload) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Invalid session" }, { status: 401 });

    await connectDB();
    const admin = await Admin.findById(payload.id).select("-passwordHash").lean();
    if (!admin || !admin.isActive) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Admin not found" }, { status: 401 });

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      data: {
        id: admin._id.toString(),
        username: admin.username,
        email: admin.email,
        role: admin.role,
        isActive: admin.isActive,
        lastLoginAt: admin.lastLoginAt,
        createdAt: admin.createdAt,
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Authentication failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

// NEW: Change Password Endpoint
export async function PUT(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;
    if (!token) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Not authenticated" }, { status: 401 });

    const payload = await verifyToken(token);
    if (!payload) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Invalid session" }, { status: 401 });

    const body = await request.json();
    const { currentPassword, newPassword } = body;

    if (!currentPassword || !newPassword) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Both current and new passwords are required" }, { status: 400 });
    }
    if (newPassword.length < 6) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "New password must be at least 6 characters" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await connectDB();
    // Must select +passwordHash to verify it
    const admin = await Admin.findById(payload.id).select("+passwordHash");
    if (!admin) return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Admin not found" }, { status: 404 });

    const isValid = await verifyPassword(currentPassword, admin.passwordHash);
    if (!isValid) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Current password is incorrect" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    admin.passwordHash = await hashPassword(newPassword);
    await admin.save();

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Password updated successfully" });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Password update error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Failed to update password" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
