FILE: src\app\api\admin\stats\route.ts
============================================================

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { verifyToken } from "@/lib/auth";
import { marketKey } from "@/lib/markets";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const token = cookieStore.get("admin_session")?.value;
    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Not authenticated" }, { status: 401 });
    }
    const payload = await verifyToken(token);
    if (!payload) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Invalid session" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    await connectDB();

    const today = new Date();
    const d = today.getDate();
    const m = today.getMonth() + 1;
    const y = today.getFullYear();

    // ONE row per table (market): keep only the LATEST entry of each market,
    // so previous-day history never doubles/triples the counts.
    const allCharts = await Chart.find({})
      .sort({ year: -1, month: -1, date: -1, updatedAt: -1 })
      .lean();

    const latestByMarket = new Map<string, (typeof allCharts)[number]>();
    for (const c of allCharts) {
      const key = marketKey(c.chartName); // Sadabahar === sadabahar
      if (!latestByMarket.has(key)) latestByMarket.set(key, c);
    }
    const markets = Array.from(latestByMarket.values());

    const isToday = (c: { date: number; month: number; year: number }) =>
      c.date === d && c.month === m && c.year === y;

    // TOTAL CHARTS = unique tables (never multiplied by history)
    const totalCharts = markets.length;
    // ACTIVE RESULTS = ONLY the CURRENT DATE's tables that are Active.
    // (A table's entry for today is always created/updated today, so this equals
    //  "tables updated on the current date" — history never counts here.)
    const totalResults = markets.filter(
    (c) => c.isActive && isToday(c) && (c.result || "").trim() !== ""
  ).length;
    // TODAY'S ENTRIES = unique tables that have an entry dated today (always ≤ Total)
    const todaysEntries = markets.filter((c) => isToday(c)).length;

    // List order: up-to-date active tables first, then the ones that need update
    markets.sort((a, b) => {
      const aNeed = !a.isActive || !isToday(a) ? 1 : 0;
      const bNeed = !b.isActive || !isToday(b) ? 1 : 0;
      if (aNeed !== bNeed) return aNeed - bNeed;
      if (isToday(a) !== isToday(b)) return isToday(a) ? -1 : 1;
      return new Date(b.updatedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.updatedAt).getTime();
    });

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      data: {
        totalCharts,
        totalResults,
        todaysEntries,
        latestCharts: markets.slice(0, 200).map((c) => ({
          id: c._id.toString(),
          chartName: c.chartName,
          result: c.result,
          date: c.date,
          month: c.month,
          year: c.year,
          isActive: c.isActive,
          createdAt: c.createdAt,
          updatedAt: c.updatedAt,
          scheduledAt: c.scheduledAt,
        })),
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Stats error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: "Failed to fetch stats" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    await connectDB();

    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
    const year = searchParams.get("year");
    const month = searchParams.get("month");

    if (!year || !month) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Year and month parameters are required" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const dates = await Chart.distinct("date", {
      year: parseInt(year),
      month: parseInt(month),
    }).sort();

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      data: dates,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching dates:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: "Failed to fetch dates" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

FILE: src\app\api\charts\route.ts
============================================================

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { slugify } from "@/lib/markets";

/**
 * Public charts endpoint.
 *
 * - `data`   : only ACTIVE charts (filtered by scope)
 * - `hidden` : slugs of markets whose LATEST entry is inactive
 *              (so the front-end can hide their rows and stop auto-backfilling).
 *
 * Query params (all optional):
 *   ?year=YYYY            restrict to that year
 *   ?month=M              restrict to that month (1–12)
 *   ?date=D               restrict to that day (1–31)
 *
 * Toggling a market's entry back to active makes it reappear — no other action needed.
 */
export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const sp = request.nextUrl.searchParams;
    const year = sp.get("year");
    const month = sp.get("month");
    const date = sp.get("date");

    const scope: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    if (year) scope.year = Number(year);
    if (month) scope.month = Number(month);
    if (date) scope.date = Number(date);

    await connectDB();

    // TEMPORARY DELETE: a market is hidden when its LATEST entry is inactive.
    // Sort newest-first so `$first` picks the newest entry's isActive flag.
    const groups = await Chart.aggregate([
      { $match: scope },
      { $sort: { year: -1, month: -1, date: -1, updatedAt: -1 } },
      { $group: { _id: "$chartName", latestActive: { $first: "$isActive" } } },
      { $match: { latestActive: false } },
    ]);
    const hidden = (groups as Array<{ _id: string }>).map((g) => slugify(String(g._id)));

    // Only ACTIVE entries reach the public front-end.
    const charts = await Chart.find({ ...scope, isActive: true })
      .sort({ displayOrder: 1, chartName: 1, date: 1 })
      .select("chartName result date month year isHighlighted isActive drawTime displayOrder updatedAt")
      .lean();

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, data: charts, hidden });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Public charts error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: "Failed to load charts", data: [], hidden: [] },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    await connectDB();

    const years = (await Chart.distinct("year")).filter((y: number) => y >= 2015);
    const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
    // Generate years from 2012 to current year only
    const allYears = Array.from({ length: currentYear - 2012 + 1 }, (_, i) => 2012 + i);
    const combinedYears = Array.from(new Set([...years, ...allYears])).sort((a, b) => b - a);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      data: combinedYears,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching years:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: "Failed to fetch years" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { z } from "zod";

const contactSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(2, "Name must be at least 2 characters"),
  email: z.string().email("Invalid email address"),
  subject: z.string().min(5, "Subject must be at least 5 characters"),
  message: z.string().min(10, "Message must be at least 10 characters"),
});

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();
    const validation = contactSchema.safeParse(body);

    if (!validation.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Invalid input", errors: validation.error.flatten().fieldErrors },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    // In production, you would send an email or save to database
    // For now, we'll just log and return success
    console.log("Contact form submission:", validation.data);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: "Message sent successfully",
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Contact form error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: "Failed to send message" },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: src\app\api\help-contact\route.ts
============================================================

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { z } from "zod";

const schema = z.object({
  type: z.enum(["tips", "agent", "advertise", "listing", "build", "suggest"]),
  lang: z.string().min(2).max(5),
  name: z.string().min(2, "Name must be at least 2 characters"),
  email: z.string().email("Invalid email").optional().or(z.literal("")),
  phone: z.string().min(5, "Telegram or WhatsApp number is required"),
  gameName: z.string().max(100).optional().or(z.literal("")),
  resultTime: z.string().max(10).optional().or(z.literal("")),
  message: z.string().max(280).optional().or(z.literal("")),
});

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();
    const validation = schema.safeParse(body);
    if (!validation.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: "Invalid input", errors: validation.error.flatten().fieldErrors },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }
    if (validation.data.type === "listing") {
      if (!validation.data.gameName || !validation.data.resultTime) {
        return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Game name and result time are required" }, { status: 400 });
      }
    }
    // In production you can email / save this to MongoDB here.
    console.log("Help contact submission:", validation.data);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Message sent successfully" });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Help contact error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Failed to send message" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: src\app\contact\page.tsx
============================================================

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Header } from "@/components/layout/Header";
import { Footer } from "@/components/layout/Footer";
import { Button } from "@/components/shared/Button";
import { Input } from "@/components/shared/Input";
import { Loading } from "@/components/shared/Loading";
import { Mail, Phone, MapPin, Send, CheckCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

export default function ContactPage() {
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    name: "",
    email: "",
    subject: "",
    message: "",
  });
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [submitStatus, setSubmitStatus] = useState<"idle" | "success" | "error">("idle");

  const validateForm = () => {
    const newErrors: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (!formData.name.trim()) newErrors.name = "Name is required";
    if (!formData.email.trim()) newErrors.email = "Email is required";
    else if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(formData.email)) newErrors.email = "Invalid email format";
    if (!formData.subject.trim()) newErrors.subject = "Subject is required";
    if (!formData.message.trim()) newErrors.message = "Message is required";
    else if (formData.message.length < 10) newErrors.message = "Message must be at least 10 characters";
    setErrors(newErrors);
    return Object.keys(newErrors).length === 0;
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!validateForm()) return;

    setIsSubmitting(true);
    setSubmitStatus("idle");

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/contact", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(formData),
      });

      if (res.ok) {
        setSubmitStatus("success");
        setFormData({ name: "", email: "", subject: "", message: "" });
      } else {
        setSubmitStatus("error");
      }
    } catch {
      setSubmitStatus("error");
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
    const { name, value } = e.target;
    setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
    if (errors[name]) setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [name]: "" }));
  };

  const announcements = {
    red: "Contact us for any inquiries or support.",
    green: "We typically respond within 24 hours.",
  };

  return (
    <div className="page-container">
      <Header announcements={announcements} />
      <main className="main-content" role="main">
        <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto">
          <header className="mb-8 text-center">
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4">Contact Us</h1>
            <p className="text-gray-600">Have questions? We'd love to hear from you.</p>
          </header>

          <div className="grid gap-8 md:grid-cols-2 mb-12">
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-4 p-4 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-lg bg-primary-100 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
                  <Mail className="w-6 h-6 text-primary-600" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-900">Email</h3>
                  <p className="text-gray-600">support@onlinekhiwal.com</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-start gap-4 p-4 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-lg bg-primary-100 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
                  <Phone className="w-6 h-6 text-primary-600" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-900">Phone</h3>
                  <p className="text-gray-600">+91 XXXXX XXXXX</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-start gap-4 p-4 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-lg bg-primary-100 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
                  <MapPin className="w-6 h-6 text-primary-600" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-900">Address</h3>
                  <p className="text-gray-600">India</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="card p-6">
              <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-6">Send a Message</h2>
              
              {submitStatus === "success" && (
                <div className="mb-6 p-4 bg-success-50 border border-success-200 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-3 text-success-700" role="alert">
                  <CheckCircle className="w-5 h-5 flex-shrink-0" />
                  <span>Thank you for your message! We'll get back to you soon.</span>
                </div>
              )}

              {submitStatus === "error" && (
                <div className="mb-6 p-4 bg-danger-50 border border-danger-200 rounded-lg text-danger-700" role="alert">
                  Failed to send message. Please try again later.
                </div>
              )}

              <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5" noValidate>
                <Input
                  label="Name"
                  name="name"
                  value={formData.name}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  error={errors.name}
                  placeholder="Your name"
                  required
                  disabled={isSubmitting}
                />
                <Input
                  label="Email"
                  name="email"
                  type="email"
                  value={formData.email}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  error={errors.email}
                  placeholder="your@email.com"
                  required
                  disabled={isSubmitting}
                />
                <Input
                  label="Subject"
                  name="subject"
                  value={formData.subject}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  error={errors.subject}
                  placeholder="Subject"
                  required
                  disabled={isSubmitting}
                />
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="message" className="label">Message</label>
                  <textarea
                    id="message"
                    name="message"
                    value={formData.message}
                    onChange={handleChange}
                    rows={5}
                    className={clsx(
                      "input resize-y min-h-[120px]",
                      errors.message && "border-danger-500 focus:border-danger-500 focus:ring-danger-500/20"
                    )}
                    placeholder="Your message..."
                    required
                    disabled={isSubmitting}
                  />
                  {errors.message && <p className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-danger-600" role="alert">{errors.message}</p>}
                </div>
                <Button type="submit" variant="primary" className="w-full" loading={isSubmitting}>
                  {isSubmitting ? <Loading size="sm" /> : <Send className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />}
                  Send Message
                </Button>
              </form>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\app\contact-form\page.tsx
============================================================

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import {
  PartyPopper, Gamepad2, Megaphone, Ticket, Code2, MessageCircle,
  CheckCircle2, User, Mail, Phone, Clock,
} from "lucide-react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";
import { LANGUAGES, T, LangCode, OptionKey } from "@/lib/contact-i18n";

type Step = "lang" | "options" | "form" | "done";
type OptionType = "tips" | "agent" | "advertise" | "listing" | "build" | "suggest";

const OPTIONS: Array<{ type: OptionType; key: OptionKey; icon: typeof User; color: string }> = [
  { type: "tips", key: "tips", icon: PartyPopper, color: "text-yellow-500" },
  { type: "agent", key: "agent", icon: Gamepad2, color: "text-sky-500" },
  { type: "advertise", key: "adv", icon: Megaphone, color: "text-amber-500" },
  { type: "listing", key: "list", icon: Ticket, color: "text-red-500" },
  { type: "build", key: "build", icon: Code2, color: "text-purple-600" },
  { type: "suggest", key: "sug", icon: MessageCircle, color: "text-green-600" },
];

const inputCls = (err?: string) =>
  clsx(
    "w-full rounded-md border bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-900 placeholder:text-gray-400",
    "focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 transition-all",
    err ? "border-red-400" : "border-gray-300"
  );

function LabelRow({ icon: Icon, label, required }: { icon: typeof User; label: string; required?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
      <Icon className="h-4 w-4 text-blue-600" aria-hidden="true" />
      <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800">{label}</span>
      {required && <span className="text-sm text-[#f0435f]">*</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ContactFormPage() {
  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>("lang");
  const [lang, setLang] = useState<LangCode>("en");
  const [type, setType] = useState<OptionType | null>(null);
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<OptionType | null>(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: "", email: "", phone: "", gameName: "", resultTime: "", message: "" });
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [sending, setSending] = useState(false);

  const t = T[lang];
  const rtl = lang === "ur";
  const isListing = type === "listing";

  const chooseOption = (ot: OptionType) => {
    if (picked) return;
    setPicked(ot);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setType(ot);
      setPicked(null);
      setStep("form");
    }, 350);
  };

  const set = (k: keyof typeof form) => (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
    setForm((p) => ({ ...p, [k]: e.target.value }));
    setErrors((p) => ({ ...p, [k]: "" }));
  };

  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const errs: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (!form.name.trim()) errs.name = "required";
    if (!form.phone.trim()) errs.phone = "required";
    if (form.email && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(form.email)) errs.email = "invalid";
    if (isListing) {
      if (!form.gameName.trim()) errs.gameName = "required";
      if (!form.resultTime) errs.resultTime = "required";
    } else if (!form.message.trim()) errs.message = "required";
    setErrors(errs);
    if (Object.keys(errs).length) return;

    setSending(true);
    try {
      await fetch("/api/help-contact", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ type, lang, ...form }),
      });
      setStep("done");
    } catch {
      setStep("done");
    } finally {
      setSending(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#eef0f2] px-4 py-10" dir={rtl ? "rtl" : "ltr"}>
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[640px] rounded-xl bg-[#f8f9fa] p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-10">

        {/* STEP 1 — SELECT LANGUAGE */}
        {step === "lang" && (
          <>
            <h1 className="mb-8 text-center text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900 sm:text-4xl">Select Language</h1>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              {LANGUAGES.map((l) => (
                <button
                  key={l.code}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => { setLang(l.code); setStep("options"); }}
                  className="rounded-md border border-blue-500 bg-white px-4 py-4 text-center font-semibold text-blue-600 transition-colors hover:bg-blue-50"
                >
                  {l.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* STEP 2 — HELP OPTIONS */}
        {step === "options" && (
          <>
            <h1 className="mb-8 text-center text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900 sm:text-4xl">{t.helpTitle}</h1>
            <div className="space-y-5">
              {OPTIONS.map((o) => {
                const Icon = o.icon;
                const isPicked = picked === o.type;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={o.type}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => chooseOption(o.type)}
                    className={clsx(
                      "w-full rounded-lg border bg-white p-5 text-left transition-colors",
                      isPicked ? "border-blue-500 bg-blue-50/60 ring-1 ring-blue-500" : "border-gray-300 hover:border-blue-400"
                    )}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <Icon className={clsx("h-6 w-6 flex-shrink-0", o.color)} aria-hidden="true" />
                        <span className="font-bold text-gray-900">{t.options[o.key].title}</span>
                      </div>
                      {isPicked ? (
                        <CheckCircle2 className="h-6 w-6 flex-shrink-0 text-blue-600" fill="currentColor" stroke="white" aria-hidden="true" />
                      ) : (
                        <CheckCircle2 className="h-6 w-6 flex-shrink-0 text-gray-300" aria-hidden="true" />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-3 border-t border-gray-200 pt-3 text-sm leading-relaxed text-gray-500">
                      {t.options[o.key].desc}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* STEP 3 — FORM */}
        {step === "form" && type && (
          <>
            <h1 className="mb-8 text-center text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900 sm:text-4xl">
              {isListing ? t.listingTitle : t.contactTitle}
            </h1>
            <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-5" noValidate>
              <div>
                <LabelRow icon={User} label={t.lblName} required />
                <input className={inputCls(errors.name)} placeholder={t.phName} value={form.name} onChange={set("name")} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <LabelRow icon={Mail} label={t.lblEmail} />
                <input type="email" className={inputCls(errors.email)} placeholder={t.phEmail} value={form.email} onChange={set("email")} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <LabelRow icon={Phone} label={t.lblPhone} required />
                <input className={inputCls(errors.phone)} placeholder={t.phPhone} value={form.phone} onChange={set("phone")} />
              </div>
              {isListing && (
                <>
                  <div>
                    <LabelRow icon={Gamepad2} label={t.lblGame} required />
                    <input className={inputCls(errors.gameName)} placeholder={t.phGame} value={form.gameName} onChange={set("gameName")} />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <LabelRow icon={Clock} label={t.lblTime} required />
                    <input type="time" className={inputCls(errors.resultTime)} value={form.resultTime} onChange={set("resultTime")} />
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
              <div>
                <LabelRow icon={MessageCircle} label={t.lblMessage} required={!isListing} />
                <textarea rows={5} maxLength={280} className={clsx(inputCls(errors.message), "resize-y")} placeholder={t.phMessage} value={form.message} onChange={set("message")} />
                <p className="mt-1 text-end text-xs text-gray-500">{form.message.length} / 280</p>
              </div>
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={sending}
                className="w-full rounded-md bg-[#1d74e8] py-3.5 font-bold text-white transition hover:brightness-110 disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {sending ? "..." : isListing ? t.btnSubmit : t.btnSend}
              </button>
            </form>
          </>
        )}

        {/* STEP 4 — SUCCESS */}
        {step === "done" && (
          <div className="py-10 text-center">
            <CheckCircle2 className="mx-auto mb-4 h-16 w-16 text-green-600" aria-hidden="true" />
            <p className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">{t.success}</p>
            <Link href="/" className="mt-6 inline-block text-sm font-medium text-blue-600 hover:underline">
              ← onlinekhiwal.com
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

@tailwind base;

  * {
    @apply border-gray-200;
  }

  body {
    @apply bg-white text-gray-900 antialiased;
    font-feature-settings:
      "rlig" 1,
      "calt" 1;
  }

  html {
    scroll-behavior: smooth;
  }
}

@layer components {
  .btn {
    @apply inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-all duration-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none;
  }

  .btn-primary {
    @apply btn bg-blue-600 text-white hover:bg-blue-700 focus:ring-blue-500;

  .btn-secondary {
    @apply btn bg-gray-100 text-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-200 focus:ring-gray-500;
  }

  .btn-danger {
    @apply btn bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-700 focus:ring-red-500;
  }

  .btn-success {
    @apply btn bg-green-600 text-white hover:bg-green-700 focus:ring-green-500;
  }

  .btn-outline {
    @apply btn border border-gray-300 bg-white text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50 focus:ring-gray-500;
  }

  .btn-ghost {
    @apply btn bg-transparent text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100 focus:ring-gray-500;
  }

  .input {
    @apply w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-gray-900 placeholder:text-gray-400 focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 transition-all duration-200;
  }

  .select {
    @apply w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-gray-900 focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 transition-all duration-200;
  }

  .label {
    @apply block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1.5;
  }

import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";
import { Inter } from "next/font/google";
import "./globals.css";

const inter = Inter({ subsets: ["latin"], variable: "--font-inter" });

export const viewport: Viewport = {
  width: "device-width",
  initialScale: 1,
  themeColor: "#ffffff",
};

const SITE_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || "https://onlinekhiwal.com";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  metadataBase: new URL(SITE_URL),
  title: {
    default: "Dhan Kuber — Live Satta Result & Monthly Charts",
    template: "%s | Dhan Kuber",
  },
  description:
    "Dhan Kuber live results and monthly record charts for Gali, Desawar, Faridabad, Ghaziabad and all regional markets. Updated daily with full历史 archive.",
  alternates: { canonical: "/" },
  robots: { index: true, follow: true },
  openGraph: {
    type: "website",
    locale: "en_IN",
    url: SITE_URL,
    siteName: "Dhan Kuber",
    title: "Dhan Kuber — Live Satta Result & Monthly Charts",
    description: "Daily results and monthly charts for Gali, Desawar, Faridabad, Ghaziabad and more.",
    images: [{ url: "/og-image.jpg", width: 1200, height: 630, alt: "Dhan Kuber Results" }],
  },
  twitter: {
    card: "summary_large_image",
    title: "Dhan Kuber — Live Satta Result & Monthly Charts",
    description: "Daily results and monthly charts.",
    images: ["/og-image.jpg"],
  },
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <html lang="en" className={`${inter.variable} antialiased`}>
      <head>
        <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
        <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossOrigin="anonymous" />
        <link rel="icon" href="/favicon.ico" sizes="any" />
        <link rel="icon" href="/icon.svg" type="image/svg+xml" />
        <link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="/apple-touch-icon.png" />
        <link rel="manifest" href="/manifest.json" />
      </head>
      <body className="min-h-screen bg-white text-gray-900">
        {children}
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

============================================================
FILE: src\app\page.tsx
============================================================

"use client";

import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { SiteHeader } from "@/components/home/SiteHeader";
import { LiveResultsTable, LiveRow, LiveSection } from "@/components/home/LiveResultsTable";
import { MonthlyChartTable } from "@/components/home/MonthlyChartTable";
import { FloatingButtons } from "@/components/home/FloatingButtons";
import { Footer } from "@/components/layout/Footer";
import {
  DEFAULT_MARKETS, MONTH_NAMES, MONTH_SHORT, MISSING_MARK, UPCOMING_MARK, MIN_YEAR,
  addDays, autoResult, buildMonthlyRows, dateKey, drawSection, firstEntryDates, fmtTimeLabel,
  fetchChartsResilient, getCachedCharts, getCachedHidden, getDrawTime, istNow, istToday,
  longDate, minutesOf, ordinal, slugify, taglineYears, weekdayOf, DrawSection,
} from "@/lib/markets";

/* ---------------------------------- types --------------------------------- */

interface ApiChart {
  id: string;
  chartName: string;
  result: string;
  date: number;
  month: number;
  year: number;
  isHighlighted: boolean;
  isActive: boolean;
  drawTime?: string | null;
  displayOrder?: number;
  updatedAt?: string;
}

type RowState = LiveRow & {
  minutes: number;
  passed: boolean;
  dbTime?: string | null;
  dbToday?: string;
  dbYesterday?: string;
  hasDb: boolean;
  dbHighlight: boolean;
  section: DrawSection;
};

/* -------------------------------- constants ------------------------------- */

/** PERMANENT special 4 — monthly chart columns, reference order. No dynamic selection. */
const MONTHLY_COLUMNS = [
  { key: "desawar", label: "DSWR" },
  { key: "faridabad", label: "FRBD" },
  { key: "ghaziabad", label: "GZBD" },
  { key: "gali", label: "GALI" },
];

const SPECIAL_NAMES = "Gali, Desawar, Ghaziabad and Faridabad";

/* --------------------------------- helpers -------------------------------- */

/** Read ?month=&year= from the address bar. */
function readSelFromUrl(): { month: number; year: number } | null {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
  const p = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  const m = parseInt(p.get("month") || "", 10);
  const y = parseInt(p.get("year") || "", 10);
  if (m >= 1 && m <= 12 && y >= MIN_YEAR && y <= 2100) return { month: m, year: y };
  return null;
}

/* ---------------------------------- page ---------------------------------- */

export default function HomePage() {
  /* ------------------------------ live IST clock ---------------------------- */
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => istNow());
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setInterval(() => setNow(istNow()), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, []);

  const today = istToday(now);
  const yesterday = addDays(today, -1);
  const nowMinutes = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
  const minuteKey = Math.floor(now.getTime() / 60000);
  const todayKey = `${today.year}-${today.month}-${today.day}`;
  const yesterdayKey = `${yesterday.year}-${yesterday.month}-${yesterday.day}`;

  /* --------------------- daily + all charts: cache-first + fetch ------------ */
  const [todayCharts, setTodayCharts] = useState<ApiChart[]>([]);
  const [yesterdayCharts, setYesterdayCharts] = useState<ApiChart[]>([]);
  const [allCharts, setAllCharts] = useState<ApiChart[]>([]);
  const [hiddenAll, setHiddenAll] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [hiddenMonth, setHiddenMonth] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [booted, setBooted] = useState(false);

  // Inject localStorage cache BEFORE first paint so rows never flash/disappear.
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const t = getCachedCharts(todayKey);
    if (t.length) setTodayCharts(t as ApiChart[]);
    const y = getCachedCharts(yesterdayKey);
    if (y.length) setYesterdayCharts(y as ApiChart[]);
    const a = getCachedCharts("all-markets");
    if (a.length) setAllCharts(a as ApiChart[]);
    setHiddenAll(getCachedHidden("all-markets"));
    setBooted(true);
  }, [todayKey, yesterdayKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    const load = async () => {
      const [t, y] = await Promise.all([
        fetchChartsResilient(`/api/charts?year=${today.year}&month=${today.month}&date=${today.day}`, todayKey),
        fetchChartsResilient(`/api/charts?year=${yesterday.year}&month=${yesterday.month}&date=${yesterday.day}`, yesterdayKey),
      ]);
      if (!alive) return;
      if (t) setTodayCharts(t as ApiChart[]);
      if (y) setYesterdayCharts(y as ApiChart[]);
    };
    load();
    const ref = setInterval(load, 60000);
    return () => { alive = false; clearInterval(ref); };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [todayKey, yesterdayKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    const loadAll = async () => {
      const d = await fetchChartsResilient(`/api/charts`, "all-markets");
      if (!alive) return;
      if (d) setAllCharts(d as ApiChart[]);
      setHiddenAll(getCachedHidden("all-markets"));
    };
    loadAll();
    const ref = setInterval(loadAll, 60000);
    return () => { alive = false; clearInterval(ref); };
  }, []);

  // Generation date per market (earliest entry) — drives the backfill rule.
  const firstEntryBySlug = useMemo(() => firstEntryDates(allCharts), [allCharts]);

  /* ------------------------------- daily rows ------------------------------- */
  const sections = useMemo<LiveSection[]>(() => {
    const bySlug = new Map<string, RowState>();
    const ensure = (name: string): RowState => {
      const slug = slugify(name);
      if (!bySlug.has(slug)) {
        bySlug.set(slug, {
          slug, name, timeLabel: "", yesterday: MISSING_MARK, today: UPCOMING_MARK,
          highlighted: false, minutes: 0, passed: false,
          hasDb: false, dbHighlight: false, section: "REST",
        });
      }
      return bySlug.get(slug)!;
    };

    const absorb = (r: RowState, c: ApiChart) => {
      r.hasDb = true;
      if (c.isHighlighted) r.dbHighlight = true;
      r.dbTime = r.dbTime || c.drawTime;
    };

    // Yesterday first (fallback time)…
    for (const c of yesterdayCharts) {
      const r = ensure(c.chartName);
      r.dbYesterday = c.result;
      absorb(r, c);
    }
    // …then TODAY: its admin-set draw time ALWAYS wins ("" = use default schedule).
    for (const c of todayCharts) {
      const r = ensure(c.chartName);
      r.dbToday = c.result;
      r.dbTime = c.drawTime || null;
      absorb(r, c);
    }
    for (const m of DEFAULT_MARKETS) ensure(m);

    // Latest active entry per market (name / draw-time / highlight).
    const meta = new Map<string, { name: string; drawTime?: string | null; highlighted: boolean; key: number }>();
    for (const c of allCharts) {
      const s = slugify(c.chartName);
      const k = dateKey(c.year, c.month, c.date);
      const prev = meta.get(s);
      if (!prev || k >= prev.key) {
        meta.set(s, { name: c.chartName, drawTime: c.drawTime, highlighted: c.isHighlighted, key: k });
      }
    }
    for (const mm of Array.from(meta.values())) {
      const r = ensure(mm.name);
      r.dbTime = r.dbTime || mm.drawTime;
      if (!r.hasDb && mm.highlighted) r.highlighted = true;
    }

    const yKey = dateKey(yesterday.year, yesterday.month, yesterday.day);
    // Markets whose entries are ALL inactive are removed entirely.
    const hiddenSet = new Set(hiddenAll);
    const list = Array.from(bySlug.values()).filter((r) => !hiddenSet.has(r.slug));

    for (const r of list) {
      if (r.hasDb) r.highlighted = r.dbHighlight;
      const time = getDrawTime(r.slug, r.dbTime);
      r.minutes = minutesOf(time);
      r.timeLabel = fmtTimeLabel(time);
      r.passed = nowMinutes >= r.minutes;
      r.section = drawSection(r.minutes, nowMinutes);

      // TODAY: hidden until the draw time. After → admin value, else "--". NEVER auto.
      r.today = r.passed ? (r.dbToday || MISSING_MARK) : UPCOMING_MARK;
      // YESTERDAY: admin value, else auto ONLY before the market's generation, else "--".
      const gen = firstEntryBySlug.get(r.slug);
      if (r.dbYesterday) r.yesterday = r.dbYesterday;
      else if (gen !== undefined && yKey < gen) r.yesterday = autoResult(r.slug, yesterday.day, yesterday.month, yesterday.year);
      else r.yesterday = MISSING_MARK;
    }

    const live = list.filter((r) => r.section === "LIVE").sort((a, b) => b.minutes - a.minutes);
    const next = list.filter((r) => r.section === "NEXT").sort((a, b) => a.minutes - b.minutes);
    const rest = list.filter((r) => r.section === "REST").sort((a, b) => a.minutes - b.minutes);
    // LIVE header is ALWAYS shown (with its pulsing icon), even before the first draw.
    return [
      { label: "LIVE", rows: live },
      { label: "NEXT", rows: next },
      { label: "REST", rows: rest },
    ].filter((s) => s.label === "LIVE" || s.rows.length > 0);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [todayCharts, yesterdayCharts, allCharts, firstEntryBySlug, minuteKey, hiddenAll]);

  /* ------------------ monthly chart: month/year live in URL ----------------- */
  const [sel, setSel] = useState<{ month: number; year: number } | null>(null);
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const fromUrl = readSelFromUrl();
    const t = istToday(istNow());
    setSel(fromUrl || { month: t.month, year: t.year });
  }, []);

  const [monthCharts, setMonthCharts] = useState<ApiChart[]>([]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sel) return;
    let alive = true;
    const key = `m-${sel.year}-${sel.month}`;
    setMonthCharts(getCachedCharts(key) as ApiChart[]); // instant from cache
    setHiddenMonth(getCachedHidden(key));
    fetchChartsResilient(`/api/charts?year=${sel.year}&month=${sel.month}`, key).then((d) => {
      if (!alive) return;
      if (d) setMonthCharts(d as ApiChart[]);
      setHiddenMonth(getCachedHidden(key));
    });
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, [sel]);

  const monthlyRows = useMemo(
    () => (sel ? buildMonthlyRows(MONTHLY_COLUMNS, sel, monthCharts, today, nowMinutes, firstEntryBySlug, hiddenMonth) : []),
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    [sel, monthCharts, firstEntryBySlug, minuteKey, today.day, hiddenMonth]
  );

  /** Full page navigation (reload) to the requested month. */
  const navigateMonth = (m: number, y: number) => {
    window.location.assign(`/?ResultFor=${MONTH_NAMES[m - 1]}-${y}&month=${m}&year=${y}#mix-chart`);
  };

  const selMonth = sel?.month ?? today.month;
  const selYear = sel?.year ?? today.year;
  const prevDate = new Date(selYear, selMonth - 2, 1);
  const nextDate = new Date(selYear, selMonth, 1);
  const clock = `${String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(now.getSeconds()).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  const tagline = `Daily Superfast Dhan Kuber Result of ${ordinal(today.day)} ${MONTH_NAMES[today.month - 1]} ${today.year} And Leak Numbers for ${SPECIAL_NAMES} With Complete Old Dhan Kuber Chart of ${taglineYears().join(", ")} From Dhan Kuber, Dhan Kuber Gali, Dhan Kuber Desawar, Dhan Kuber Ghaziabad, Dhan Kuber Faridabad.`;

  /* --------------------------------- render -------------------------------- */
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col bg-[#ececec]">
      <SiteHeader tagline={tagline} />

      <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1200px] flex-1 px-4 pb-16">
        <p suppressHydrationWarning className="mt-4 text-center text-sm text-[#16a34a]">
          Updated: {MONTH_NAMES[now.getMonth()]} {now.getDate()}, {now.getFullYear()}, {clock} IST.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-6 space-y-6">
          <LiveResultsTable
            banner={`Dhan Kuber Results of ${longDate(today)} & ${longDate(yesterday)}`}
            col1={`${weekdayOf(yesterday)}. ${ordinal(yesterday.day)}`}
            col2={`${weekdayOf(today)}. ${ordinal(today.day)}`}
            sections={booted ? sections : []}
            showFooter
          />

          <div className="bg-[#1565d8] px-4 py-3 text-center text-sm text-white sm:text-base">
            <span className="mr-1 font-bold text-[#ff8a80]">New:</span>
            Show your game at its original position of the next day
          </div>

          <div id="mix-chart">
            <MonthlyChartTable
              title={`Monthly Dhan Kuber Result Chart of ${MONTH_NAMES[selMonth - 1]} ${selYear} for ${SPECIAL_NAMES}`}
              columns={MONTHLY_COLUMNS}
              rows={monthlyRows}
              prevLabel={`${MONTH_SHORT[prevDate.getMonth()]} ${prevDate.getFullYear()}`}
              nextLabel={`${MONTH_SHORT[nextDate.getMonth()]} ${nextDate.getFullYear()}`}
              onPrev={() => navigateMonth(prevDate.getMonth() + 1, prevDate.getFullYear())}
              onNext={() => navigateMonth(nextDate.getMonth() + 1, nextDate.getFullYear())}
              latestLabel={`Click here to view the latest chart for ${SPECIAL_NAMES} for ${MONTH_NAMES[today.month - 1]}-${today.year}`}
              onLatest={() => navigateMonth(today.month, today.year)}
              selectorLabel={`Select a month and year to view the combined chart for ${SPECIAL_NAMES}.`}
              selectedMonth={selMonth}
              selectedYear={selYear}
              onGo={(m, y) => navigateMonth(m, y)}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>

      <Footer />
      <FloatingButtons />
    </div>
  );
}

============================================================
FILE: src\app\privacy\page.tsx

import { Metadata } from "next";
import { Header } from "@/components/layout/Header";
import { Footer } from "@/components/layout/Footer";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Privacy Policy",
  description: "Privacy Policy for Dhan Kuber website",
};

export default function PrivacyPage() {
  const announcements = {
    red: "Your privacy is important to us. Please review our privacy practices.",
    green: "Updated: Privacy Policy last updated on January 1, 2025.",

import type { MetadataRoute } from "next";

const BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || "https://onlinekhiwal.com";

export default function robots(): MetadataRoute.Robots {
  return {
    rules: [{ userAgent: "*", allow: "/", disallow: ["/admin/", "/api/"] }],
    sitemap: `${BASE}/sitemap.xml`,
    host: BASE,
  };
}

============================================================
FILE: src\app\sitemap.ts
============================================================

import type { MetadataRoute } from "next";
import connectDB from "@/lib/mongodb";
import Chart from "@/models/Chart";
import { slugify } from "@/lib/markets";

const BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || "https://onlinekhiwal.com";
export const revalidate = 3600;

export default async function sitemap(): Promise<MetadataRoute.Sitemap> {
  const statics: MetadataRoute.Sitemap = ["", "/contact-form", "/terms", "/privacy", "/disclaimer"].map((p) => ({
    url: `${BASE}${p}`,
    lastModified: new Date(),
    changeFrequency: "daily",
    priority: p === "" ? 1 : 0.4,
  }));

  try {
    await connectDB();
    const names = (await Chart.distinct("chartName")) as string[];
    const markets: MetadataRoute.Sitemap = names.map((n) => ({
      url: `${BASE}/${slugify(n)}/satta-result-chart/`,
      lastModified: new Date(),
      changeFrequency: "daily",
      priority: 0.8,
    }));
    return [...statics, ...markets];
  } catch {
    return statics;
  }
}

FILE: src\app\[market]\satta-result-chart\[[...rest]]\page.tsx
============================================================

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { MarketChartClient } from "@/components/home/MarketChartClient";
import { MONTH_NAMES, istNow, istToday, titleFromSlug } from "@/lib/markets";

type Props = { params: { market: string } };

/** Unique SEO title/description per market — the whole point of the split. */
export function generateMetadata({ params }: Props): Metadata {
  const name = titleFromSlug(params.market);
  const t = istToday(istNow());
  const month = MONTH_NAMES[t.month - 1];
  const title = `${name} Satta Result Chart ${month} ${t.year}`;
  const description = `${name} daily satta result and monthly record chart for ${month} ${t.year}, with Gali, Desawar, Faridabad and Ghaziabad combined charts on Dhan Kuber.`;
  const url = `/${params.market}/satta-result-chart/`;
  return {
    title,
    description,
    alternates: { canonical: url },
    openGraph: { title, description, url, type: "article" },
  };
}

export default function MarketChartPage({ params }: Props) {
  return <MarketChartClient slug={params.market} />;
}

FILE: src\components\admin\AdminSidebar.tsx
============================================================

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { clsx } from "clsx";
import {
  LayoutDashboard, BarChart2, PlusCircle, Users, Settings, LogOut,
  ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, X,
} from "lucide-react";

interface AdminSidebarProps {
  isCollapsed?: boolean;
  onToggle?: () => void;
  user?: { username: string; role: string };
}

const navItems = [
  { href: "/admin/dashboard", label: "Dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { href: "/admin/charts", label: "Charts", icon: BarChart2 },
  { href: "/admin/charts/add", label: "Add Chart", icon: PlusCircle },
  { href: "/admin/admins", label: "Admins", icon: Users, roles: ["super_admin"] },
  { href: "/admin/settings", label: "Settings", icon: Settings },
];

/** Custom event fired by the header hamburger (keeps pages untouched). */
export const SIDEBAR_TOGGLE_EVENT = "admin-sidebar-toggle";

export function AdminSidebar({ isCollapsed = false, onToggle, user }: AdminSidebarProps) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);

  // Open/close from the header hamburger.
  useEffect(() => {
    const toggle = () => setMobileOpen((v) => !v);
    window.addEventListener(SIDEBAR_TOGGLE_EVENT, toggle);
    return () => window.removeEventListener(SIDEBAR_TOGGLE_EVENT, toggle);
  }, []);

  // Auto-close the drawer whenever the route changes.
  useEffect(() => {
    setMobileOpen(false);
  }, [pathname]);

  const filteredItems = navItems.filter((i) => !i.roles || i.roles.includes(user?.role || ""));

  return (
    <>
      {/* Mobile backdrop */}
      {mobileOpen && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-black/50 lg:hidden"
          onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
          aria-hidden="true"
        />
      )}

      <aside
        data-collapsed={isCollapsed ? "true" : "false"}
        className={clsx(
          "admin-sidebar bg-white border-r border-gray-200 flex flex-col transition-all duration-300 w-64",
          isCollapsed ? "lg:w-16" : "lg:w-64",
          mobileOpen ? "translate-x-0" : "-translate-x-full lg:translate-x-0"
        )}
        aria-label="Admin navigation"
      >
        {/* header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between h-16 px-4 border-b border-gray-200">
          <Link href="/admin/dashboard" className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0" aria-label="Admin Dashboard">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-primary-600 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
              <span className="text-white font-bold text-lg">OK</span>
            </div>
            {!isCollapsed && <span className="font-bold text-xl text-gray-900 truncate">Admin Panel</span>}
          </Link>

          {/* mobile: close drawer */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
            className="lg:hidden p-1.5 rounded-lg text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100"
            aria-label="Close menu"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>

          {/* desktop: collapse rail */}
          {onToggle && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onToggle}
              className="hidden lg:flex p-1.5 rounded-lg text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100"
              aria-label={isCollapsed ? "Expand sidebar" : "Collapse sidebar"}
              aria-expanded={!isCollapsed}
            >
              {isCollapsed ? <ChevronRight className="w-5 h-5" /> : <ChevronLeft className="w-5 h-5" />}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* nav */}
        <nav className="flex-1 px-3 py-4 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto" aria-label="Main admin menu">
          {filteredItems.map((item) => {
            const isActive = pathname === item.href || (item.href !== "/admin/dashboard" && pathname.startsWith(item.href));
            return (
              <Link
                key={item.href}
                href={item.href}
                onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
                className={clsx(
                  "flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-all duration-200",
                  isActive ? "bg-primary-50 text-primary-700" : "text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:text-gray-900",
                  isCollapsed && "lg:justify-center"
                )}
                aria-current={isActive ? "page" : undefined}
                title={isCollapsed ? item.label : undefined}
              >
                <item.icon className="w-5 h-5 flex-shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                {!isCollapsed && <span>{item.label}</span>}
              </Link>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        {/* footer */}
        <div className="p-4 border-t border-gray-200">
          <div className={clsx("flex items-center gap-3", isCollapsed && "lg:justify-center")}>
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-primary-100 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
              <span className="text-primary-700 font-medium text-sm">
                {user?.username?.charAt(0).toUpperCase() || "A"}
              </span>
            </div>
            {!isCollapsed && (
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900 truncate">{user?.username}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 capitalize">{user?.role?.replace("_", " ")}</p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          {!isCollapsed && (
            <form action="/admin/logout" method="POST" className="mt-4">
              <button
                type="submit"
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 hover:text-danger-600 hover:bg-danger-50 rounded-lg transition-colors"
              >
                <LogOut className="w-5 h-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>Sign Out</span>
              </button>
            </form>
          )}
        </div>
      </aside>
    </>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";
import { Button } from "@/components/shared/Button";
import { Select } from "@/components/shared/Select";
import { DrawTimePicker } from "@/components/admin/DrawTimePicker";
import { Tag, Hash, CalendarClock, Eye, Star, CalendarDays } from "lucide-react";
import { fmtTimeLabel, getDrawTime, slugify } from "@/lib/markets";
import { SPECIAL_MARKETS, marketKey } from "@/lib/markets";

interface ChartFormData {
  chartName: string;
  result: string;
  date: number;
  month: number;
  year: number;
  displayOrder: number;
  isHighlighted: boolean;
  isActive: boolean;
  scheduledAt?: string; // ISO datetime string
  drawTime?: string; // 24h "HH:MM"
}

interface ChartFormProps {
  initialData?: Partial<ChartFormData>;
  onSubmit: (data: ChartFormData) => Promise<void>;
  onCancel: () => void;
  isLoading?: boolean;
  availableYears?: number[];
  availableMonths?: number[];
  availableDates?: number[];
}

const DEFAULT_DATA: ChartFormData = {
  chartName: "",
  result: "",
  date: new Date().getDate(),
  month: new Date().getMonth() + 1,
  year: new Date().getFullYear(),
  displayOrder: 0,
  isHighlighted: false,
  isActive: true,
  scheduledAt: "",
  drawTime: "",
};

function buildInitial(initial?: Partial<ChartFormData>): ChartFormData {
  if (!initial) return { ...DEFAULT_DATA };
  return {
    chartName: initial.chartName ?? DEFAULT_DATA.chartName,
    result: initial.result ?? DEFAULT_DATA.result,
    date: initial.date ?? DEFAULT_DATA.date,
    month: initial.month ?? DEFAULT_DATA.month,
    year: initial.year ?? DEFAULT_DATA.year,
    displayOrder: initial.displayOrder ?? DEFAULT_DATA.displayOrder,
    isHighlighted: initial.isHighlighted ?? DEFAULT_DATA.isHighlighted,
    isActive: initial.isActive ?? DEFAULT_DATA.isActive,
    scheduledAt: initial.scheduledAt ?? DEFAULT_DATA.scheduledAt,
    drawTime: initial.drawTime ?? DEFAULT_DATA.drawTime,
  };
}

  { value: 9, label: "September" },

];

const COMMON_CHARTS = [
