FILE: src\components\home\MonthlyChartTable.tsx
============================================================

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { MONTH_NAMES, yearOptions } from "@/lib/markets";

export interface MonthlyColumn { key: string; label: string; }
export interface MonthlyRow { date: number; values: Record<string, string>; red: boolean; }

interface MonthlyChartTableProps {
  title: string;
  columns: MonthlyColumn[];
  rows: MonthlyRow[];
  prevLabel: string;
  nextLabel: string;
  onPrev: () => void;
  onNext: () => void;
  latestLabel: string;
  onLatest: () => void;
  selectorLabel: string;
  selectedMonth: number;
  selectedYear: number;
  onGo: (month: number, year: number) => void;
}

export function MonthlyChartTable({
  title, columns, rows, prevLabel, nextLabel, onPrev, onNext,
  latestLabel, onLatest, selectorLabel, selectedMonth, selectedYear, onGo,
}: MonthlyChartTableProps) {
  const [m, setM] = useState(selectedMonth);
  const [y, setY] = useState(selectedYear);

  useEffect(() => {
    setM(selectedMonth);
    setY(selectedYear);
  }, [selectedMonth, selectedYear]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="bg-[#3ed99c] px-4 py-3 text-center text-sm text-white sm:text-base">{title}</div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full border-collapse bg-white text-center text-sm">
          <thead>
              <tr className="bg-[#f2c900] text-gray-900">
                <th className="w-16 border border-gray-300 px-2 py-2 font-bold text-[#e53935]">DATE</th>
                {columns.map((c) => (
                  <th
                    key={c.key}
                    className="min-w-[120px] border border-gray-300 px-2 py-2 text-[13px] font-bold leading-tight whitespace-normal sm:text-sm"
                    title={c.label}
                  >
                    {c.label}
                  </th>
                ))}
              </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {rows.length === 0 ? (
              <tr>
                <td
                  colSpan={columns.length + 1}
                  className="border border-gray-200 bg-white px-2 py-6 text-center text-sm text-gray-600"
                >
                  No results declared yet for this month. Rows start automatically from day 1 at 12:00 AM IST.
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : (
              rows.map((r) => (
                <tr key={r.date} className={r.date % 2 === 0 ? "bg-[#f4f6fa]" : "bg-white"}>
                  <td className={`border border-gray-200 px-2 py-1.5 font-semibold ${r.red ? "text-[#e53935]" : "text-[#e91e63]"}`}>
                    {r.date.toString().padStart(2, "0")}
                  </td>
                  {columns.map((c) => (
                    <td key={c.key} className={`border border-gray-200 px-2 py-1.5 font-medium ${r.red ? "text-[#e53935]" : "text-gray-900"}`}>
                      {r.values[c.key] ?? "XX"}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              ))
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-24 gap-y-3">
        <button type="button" onClick={onPrev} className="bg-[#1565d8] px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:brightness-110">
          {prevLabel}
        </button>
        <button type="button" onClick={onNext} className="bg-[#1565d8] px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:brightness-110">
          {nextLabel}
        </button>
      </div>

      <button type="button" onClick={onLatest} className="w-full bg-[#1565d8] px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:brightness-110">
        {latestLabel}
      </button>

      <div className="bg-[#f2c900] px-4 py-4 text-center">
        <p className="mb-3 text-sm text-gray-900">{selectorLabel}</p>
        <div className="flex flex-col items-stretch justify-center gap-3 sm:flex-row">
          <select
            value={m}
            onChange={(e) => setM(Number(e.target.value))}
            className="flex-1 border border-gray-400 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-gray-900"
            aria-label="Select month"
          >
            {MONTH_NAMES.map((n, i) => (
              <option key={n} value={i + 1}>{n}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <select
            value={y}
            onChange={(e) => setY(Number(e.target.value))}
            className="flex-1 border border-gray-400 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-gray-900"
            aria-label="Select year"
          >
            {yearOptions().map((yr) => (
              <option key={yr} value={yr}>{yr}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => onGo(m, y)} className="flex-1 bg-[#1565d8] px-8 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:brightness-110">
            Go
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\home\ResultRow.tsx

"use client";

import { clsx } from "clsx";

interface ResultRowProps {
  chartName: string;
  result: string;
  openingResult?: string;
  closingResult?: string;
  date: number;
  month: number;
  year: number;
  isHighlighted?: boolean;
  showDate?: boolean;
  updatedAt?: string;
}

export function ResultRow({
  chartName,
  result,
  openingResult,
  closingResult,
  date,
  month,
  year,
  isHighlighted = false,
  showDate = true,
  updatedAt,
}: ResultRowProps) {
  const formattedDate = `${date.toString().padStart(2, "0")}/${month.toString().padStart(2, "0")}/${year}`;
  const formattedUpdated = updatedAt ? new Date(updatedAt).toLocaleString() : "-";

  return (
    <tr className={clsx(isHighlighted && "highlighted")}>
      <td className="font-medium text-gray-900">{chartName}</td>
      {showDate && (
        <td className="text-gray-600 whitespace-nowrap">{formattedDate}</td>
      )}
      {openingResult && (
        <td className="text-gray-700 font-mono">{openingResult}</td>
      )}
      <td className="font-bold text-lg text-primary-700 font-mono">{result}</td>
      {closingResult && (
        <td className="text-gray-700 font-mono">{closingResult}</td>
      )}
      <td className="text-gray-500 text-sm whitespace-nowrap">{formattedUpdated}</td>
    </tr>
  );
}

export function ResultRowSkeleton({ columns = 5 }: { columns?: number }) {
  return (
    <tr>
      {Array.from({ length: columns }).map((_, i) => (
        <td key={i} className="px-4 py-3">
          <div className="h-4 bg-gray-200 rounded animate-pulse w-full" />
        </td>
      ))}
    </tr>
  );
}

          </div>

            title="Loading..."
            charts={[]}
            isLoading={true}
          />
        </div>
      ))}

  );

FILE: src\components\home\ResultsTable.tsx
============================================================

"use client";

import { ResultRow, ResultRowSkeleton } from "./ResultRow";
import { Loading } from "@/components/shared/Loading";

interface ChartData {
  id: string;
  chartName: string;
  result: string;
  openingResult?: string;
  closingResult?: string;
  date: number;
  month: number;
  year: number;
  isHighlighted: boolean;
  updatedAt?: string;
  scheduledAt?: string;
}

interface ResultsTableProps {
  title: string;
  charts: ChartData[];
  isLoading?: boolean;
  showDate?: boolean;
  emptyMessage?: string;
  highlightToday?: boolean;
}

export function ResultsTable({
  title,
  charts,
  isLoading = false,
  showDate = true,
  emptyMessage = "No results available for the selected period",
  highlightToday = false,
}: ResultsTableProps) {
  const hasOpening = charts.some((c) => c.openingResult);
  const hasClosing = charts.some((c) => c.closingResult);

  const columns = [
    { key: "chartName", label: "Market / Chart" },
    ...(showDate ? [{ key: "date", label: "Date" }] : []),
    ...(hasOpening ? [{ key: "opening", label: "Open" }] : []),
    { key: "result", label: "Result" },
    ...(hasClosing ? [{ key: "closing", label: "Close" }] : []),
    { key: "updated", label: "Last Updated" },
  ];

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="table-container">
        <table className="table" role="table">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              {columns.map((col) => (
                <th key={col.key} scope="col">{col.label}</th>
              ))}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
              <ResultRowSkeleton key={i} columns={columns.length} />
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (charts.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="card p-8 text-center">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 mx-auto mb-4 bg-gray-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
          <svg className="w-8 h-8 text-gray-400" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={1.5} d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z" />
          </svg>
        </div>
        <p className="text-gray-600">{emptyMessage}</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="card overflow-hidden">
      <div className="px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-200 bg-gray-50">
        <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-900">{title}</h3>
      </div>
      <div className="table-container">
        <table className="table" role="table">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              {columns.map((col) => (
                <th key={col.key} scope="col">{col.label}</th>
              ))}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {charts.map((chart) => (
              <ResultRow
                key={chart.id}
                chartName={chart.chartName}
                result={chart.result}
                openingResult={chart.openingResult}
                closingResult={chart.closingResult}
                date={chart.date}
                month={chart.month}
                year={chart.year}
                isHighlighted={chart.isHighlighted}
                showDate={showDate}
                updatedAt={chart.updatedAt}
              />
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\home\YearSelector.tsx

"use client";

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { Calendar, ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

interface YearSelectorProps {
  selectedYear: number;
  onYearChange: (year: number) => void;
  availableYears: number[];
  disabled?: boolean;
  label?: string;
  showMonth?: boolean;
  selectedMonth?: number;
  onMonthChange?: (month: number) => void;
  availableMonths?: number[];
}

const MONTHS = [
  { value: 1, label: "January", short: "Jan" },
  { value: 2, label: "February", short: "Feb" },
  { value: 3, label: "March", short: "Mar" },
  { value: 4, label: "April", short: "Apr" },
  { value: 5, label: "May", short: "May" },
  { value: 6, label: "June", short: "Jun" },
  { value: 7, label: "July", short: "Jul" },
  { value: 8, label: "August", short: "Aug" },
  { value: 9, label: "September", short: "Sep" },
  { value: 10, label: "October", short: "Oct" },
  { value: 11, label: "November", short: "Nov" },
  { value: 12, label: "December", short: "Dec" },
];

export function YearSelector({
  selectedYear,
  onYearChange,
  availableYears,
  disabled = false,
  label = "Select Year",
  showMonth = false,
  selectedMonth,
  onMonthChange,
  availableMonths = [],
}: YearSelectorProps) {
  const [isYearOpen, setIsYearOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isMonthOpen, setIsMonthOpen] = useState(false);
  const yearDropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const monthDropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const yearButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const monthButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(event: MouseEvent) {
      const isYearClick = yearDropdownRef.current?.contains(event.target as Node) || yearButtonRef.current?.contains(event.target as Node);
      const isMonthClick = monthDropdownRef.current?.contains(event.target as Node) || monthButtonRef.current?.contains(event.target as Node);

      if (!isYearClick) setIsYearOpen(false);
      if (!isMonthClick) setIsMonthOpen(false);
    }

    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
  // Generate years from 2012 to current year only (no future years)
  const allYears = Array.from({ length: currentYear - 2012 + 1 }, (_, i) => 2012 + i);
  const years = Array.from(new Set([...availableYears, ...allYears])).sort((a, b) => b - a);

  const handleYearSelect = (year: number) => {
    onYearChange(year);
    setIsYearOpen(false);
  };

  const handleMonthSelect = (month: number) => {
    onMonthChange?.(month);
    setIsMonthOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3" role="group" aria-label="Date selection">
      <div className="relative flex-1" role="combobox" aria-expanded={isYearOpen} aria-haspopup="listbox">
        <label htmlFor="year-selector" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1.5">
          {label}
        </label>
        <button
          ref={yearButtonRef}
          type="button"
          id="year-selector"
          onClick={() => !disabled && setIsYearOpen(!isYearOpen)}
          disabled={disabled}
          className={clsx(
            "w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border bg-white text-left text-sm text-gray-900",
            "focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500/20 focus:border-primary-500",
            "flex items-center justify-between gap-2",
            disabled ? "opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed border-gray-200" : "border-gray-300 hover:border-gray-400"
          )}
          aria-label={label}
          aria-controls="year-options"
        >
          <Calendar className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
          <span className="flex-1 truncate">{selectedYear}</span>
          <ChevronDown className={clsx("w-5 h-5 text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0 transition-transform", isYearOpen && "rotate-180")} aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>

        {isYearOpen && !disabled && (
          <div
            ref={yearDropdownRef}
            id="year-options"
            role="listbox"
            className="absolute z-50 mt-1 w-full max-h-60 overflow-auto rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white shadow-lg ring-1 ring-black/5 animate-slide-down"
            aria-label="Available years"
          >
            <ul role="listbox">
              {years.map((year) => (
                <li key={year} role="option" aria-selected={year === selectedYear}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleYearSelect(year)}
                    className={clsx(
                      "w-full px-4 py-2 text-left text-sm transition-colors",
                      year === selectedYear
                        ? "bg-primary-50 text-primary-700 font-medium"
                        : "text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50",
                      year === currentYear && "font-bold"
                    )}
                  >
                    {year}
                    {year === currentYear && <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-primary-500">(Current)</span>}
                  </button>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {showMonth && (
        <div className="relative flex-1" role="combobox" aria-expanded={isMonthOpen} aria-haspopup="listbox">
          <label htmlFor="month-selector" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1.5">
            Month
          </label>
          <button
            ref={monthButtonRef}
            type="button"
            id="month-selector"
            onClick={() => !disabled && setIsMonthOpen(!isMonthOpen)}
            disabled={disabled}
            className={clsx(
              "w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border bg-white text-left text-sm text-gray-900",
              "focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500/20 focus:border-primary-500",
              "flex items-center justify-between gap-2",
              disabled ? "opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed border-gray-200" : "border-gray-300 hover:border-gray-400"
            )}
            aria-label="Select month"
            aria-controls="month-options"
          >
            <Calendar className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span className="flex-1 truncate">
              {selectedMonth ? MONTHS.find((m) => m.value === selectedMonth)?.label : "Select Month"}
            </span>
            <ChevronDown className={clsx("w-5 h-5 text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0 transition-transform", isMonthOpen && "rotate-180")} aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>

          {isMonthOpen && !disabled && (
            <div
              ref={monthDropdownRef}
              id="month-options"
              role="listbox"
              className="absolute z-50 mt-1 w-full max-h-60 overflow-auto rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white shadow-lg ring-1 ring-black/5 animate-slide-down"
              aria-label="Available months"
            >
              <ul role="listbox">
                {MONTHS.filter((m) => availableMonths.length === 0 || availableMonths.includes(m.value)).map((month) => (
                  <li key={month.value} role="option" aria-selected={month.value === selectedMonth}>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleMonthSelect(month.value)}
                      className={clsx(
                        "w-full px-4 py-2 text-left text-sm transition-colors",
                        month.value === selectedMonth
                          ? "bg-primary-50 text-primary-700 font-medium"
                          : "text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50"
                      )}
                    >
                      {month.label}
                    </button>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
const maxYear = currentYear;

export function YearInput({
  value,
  onChange,
  min = 2012,
  max = maxYear,
  label = "Year",
  error,
}: {
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  min?: number;
  max?: number;
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="w-full">
      <label htmlFor="year-input" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1.5">
        {label}
      </label>
      <input
        id="year-input"
        type="number"
        min={min}
        max={max}
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        className="input w-32"
        placeholder="YYYY"
        aria-invalid={error ? "true" : "false"}
      />
      {error && <p className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-danger-600" role="alert">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

interface NavigationProps {
  announcements?: {
    red?: string;
    green?: string;
  };
}

export function Navigation({ announcements }: NavigationProps) {
  const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const navItems = [
    { href: "/", label: "Home" },
    { href: "/#results", label: "Results" },
    { href: "/#charts", label: "Charts" },
    { href: "/terms", label: "Terms" },
    { href: "/privacy", label: "Privacy" },
    { href: "/contact", label: "Contact" },
    { href: "/disclaimer", label: "Disclaimer" },
  ];

  return (
    <header className="bg-white border-b border-gray-200 sticky top-0 z-40">
      {announcements?.red && (
        <div className="announcement-red" role="status" aria-live="polite">
          {announcements.red}
        </div>
      )}
      {announcements?.green && (
        <div className="announcement-green" role="status" aria-live="polite">
          {announcements.green}
        </div>
      )}

      <nav className="container mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8" aria-label="Main navigation">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between h-16">
          <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2" aria-label="Dhan Kuber Home">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-primary-600 flex items-center justify-center">
              <span className="text-white font-bold text-lg">OK</span>
            </div>
            <span className="font-bold text-xl text-gray-900 hidden sm:block">Dhan Kuber</span>
          </Link>

          <div className={clsx("md:flex items-center gap-6 transition-all duration-300", isMenuOpen ? "flex" : "hidden")}>
            {navItems.map((item) => (
              <Link
                key={item.href}
                href={item.href}
                className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:text-primary-600 transition-colors md:block hidden"
                onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen(false)}
              >
                {item.label}
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="md:hidden p-2 rounded-lg text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100"
            onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen(!isMenuOpen)}
            aria-label={isMenuOpen ? "Close menu" : "Open menu"}
            aria-expanded={isMenuOpen}
          >
            <svg className="w-6 h-6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
              {isMenuOpen ? (
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12" />
              ) : (
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M4 6h16M4 12h16M4 18h16" />
              )}
            </svg>
          </button>
        </div>

        {isMenuOpen && (
          <div className="md:hidden py-4 border-t border-gray-200 animate-slide-down">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              {navItems.map((item) => (
                <Link
                  key={item.href}
                  href={item.href}
                  className="px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:text-primary-600 hover:bg-gray-50 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                  onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen(false)}
                >
                  {item.label}
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </nav>
    </header>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\shared\Button.tsx

"use client";

import { forwardRef, ButtonHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

interface ButtonProps extends ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: "primary" | "secondary" | "danger" | "success" | "outline" | "ghost";
  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
  loading?: boolean;
}

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant = "primary", size = "md", loading, disabled, children, ...props }, ref) => {
    const baseStyles = "inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg font-medium transition-all duration-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none";

    const variants = {
      primary: "bg-blue-600 text-white hover:bg-blue-700 focus:ring-blue-500",
      secondary: "bg-gray-100 text-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-200 focus:ring-gray-500",
      danger: "bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-700 focus:ring-red-500",
      success: "bg-green-600 text-white hover:bg-green-700 focus:ring-green-500",
      outline: "border border-gray-300 bg-white text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50 focus:ring-gray-500",
      ghost: "bg-transparent text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100 focus:ring-gray-500",
    };

    const sizes = {
      sm: "px-3 py-1.5 text-xs",
      md: "px-4 py-2 text-sm",
      lg: "px-6 py-3 text-base",
    };

    return (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        className={clsx(baseStyles, variants[variant], sizes[size], className)}
        disabled={disabled || loading}
        {...props}
      >
        {loading && (
          <svg className="animate-spin -ml-1 mr-2 h-4 w-4" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <circle className="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4" />
            <path className="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z" />
          </svg>
        )}
        {children}
      </button>
    );
  }
);

Button.displayName = "Button";

FILE: src\components\shared\Input.tsx
============================================================

"use client";

import { forwardRef, InputHTMLAttributes, LabelHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

interface InputProps extends InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
  helperText?: string;
}

export const Input = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, InputProps>(
  ({ className, label, error, helperText, id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const inputId = id || label?.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "-");

    return (
      <div className="w-full">
        {label && (
          <label htmlFor={inputId} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1.5">
            {label}
          </label>
        )}
        <input
          ref={ref}
          id={inputId}
          className={clsx(
            "w-full rounded-lg border bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-gray-900 placeholder:text-gray-400 transition-all duration-200",
            error
              ? "border-danger-500 focus:border-danger-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-danger-500/20"
              : "border-gray-300 focus:border-primary-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500/20",
            className
          )}
          aria-invalid={error ? "true" : "false"}
          aria-describedby={error ? `${inputId}-error` : helperText ? `${inputId}-helper` : undefined}
          {...props}
        />
        {error && (
          <p id={`${inputId}-error`} className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-danger-600" role="alert">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
        {helperText && !error && (
          <p id={`${inputId}-helper`} className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-gray-500">
            {helperText}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }
);

Input.displayName = "Input";

export const Label = forwardRef<HTMLLabelElement, LabelHTMLAttributes<HTMLLabelElement>>(
  ({ className, children, ...props }, ref) => (
    <label ref={ref} className={clsx("block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1.5", className)} {...props}>
      {children}
    </label>
  )
);

Label.displayName = "Label";

FILE: src\components\shared\Loading.tsx

"use client";

import { clsx } from "clsx";

interface LoadingProps {
  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
  className?: string;
  text?: string;
  overlay?: boolean;
}

export function Loading({ size = "md", className, text, overlay = false }: LoadingProps) {
  const sizes = {
    sm: "w-4 h-4 border-2",
    md: "w-8 h-8 border-3",
    lg: "w-12 h-12 border-4",
  };

  const content = (
    <div className={clsx("flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3", className)}>
      <div
        className={clsx(
          "rounded-full border-primary-600 border-t-transparent animate-spin",
          sizes[size]
        )}
        role="status"
        aria-label="Loading"
      />
      {text && <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">{text}</p>}
    </div>
  );

  if (overlay) {
    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-white/80 z-50 flex items-center justify-center">
        {content}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return content;
}

export function TableLoading({ rows = 5, columns = 6 }: { rows?: number; columns?: number }) {
  return (
    <tbody>
      {Array.from({ length: rows }).map((_, rowIndex) => (
        <tr key={rowIndex}>
          {Array.from({ length: columns }).map((_, colIndex) => (
            <td key={colIndex} className="px-4 py-3">
              <div className="h-4 bg-gray-200 rounded animate-pulse w-full" />
            </td>
          ))}
        </tr>
      ))}
    </tbody>
  );
}

export function CardLoading({ count = 3 }: { count?: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
      {Array.from({ length: count }).map((_, i) => (
        <div key={i} className="card p-6">
          <div className="h-4 bg-gray-200 rounded animate-pulse w-3/4 mb-4" />
          <div className="h-8 bg-gray-200 rounded animate-pulse w-1/2 mb-2" />
          <div className="h-4 bg-gray-200 rounded animate-pulse w-full" />
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function PageLoading() {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center">
      <Loading size="lg" text="Loading..." />
    </div>
  );
}

============================================================
FILE: src\components\shared\Modal.tsx
============================================================

"use client";

import { Fragment, ReactNode, useEffect } from "react";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";

interface ModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title?: string;
  description?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "full";
  showCloseButton?: boolean;
  closeOnOverlayClick?: boolean;
  closeOnEscape?: boolean;
}

export function Modal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  title,
  description,
  children,
  size = "md",
  showCloseButton = true,
  closeOnOverlayClick = true,
  closeOnEscape = true,
}: ModalProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const handleEscape = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape" && closeOnEscape) {
        onClose();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleEscape);
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleEscape);
      document.body.style.overflow = "unset";
    };
  }, [isOpen, closeOnEscape, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const sizes = {
    sm: "max-w-md",
    md: "max-w-lg",
    lg: "max-w-2xl",
    xl: "max-w-4xl",
    full: "max-w-full mx-4",
  };

  const modalContent = (
    <Fragment>
      <div
        className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 animate-fade-in"
        onClick={closeOnOverlayClick ? onClose : undefined}
        aria-hidden="true"
      />
      <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-slide-up">
        <div
          className={clsx(
            "w-full bg-white rounded-xl shadow-xl overflow-hidden",
            sizes[size]
          )}
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
          aria-labelledby={title ? "modal-title" : undefined}
          aria-describedby={description ? "modal-description" : undefined}
        >
          {(title || showCloseButton) && (
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between p-4 border-b border-gray-200">
              <div>
                {title && (
                  <h2 id="modal-title" className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">
                    {title}
                  </h2>
                )}
                {description && (
                  <p id="modal-description" className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
                    {description}
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
              {showCloseButton && (
                <button
                  onClick={onClose}
                  className="p-1 rounded-lg text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
                  aria-label="Close modal"
                >
                  <X className="w-5 h-5" />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="p-4">{children}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Fragment>
  );

  if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;

  return createPortal(modalContent, document.body);
}

interface ConfirmModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onConfirm: () => void;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  confirmText?: string;
  cancelText?: string;
  variant?: "danger" | "primary";
  loading?: boolean;
}

export function ConfirmModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onConfirm,
  title,
  message,
  confirmText = "Confirm",
  cancelText = "Cancel",
  variant = "danger",
  loading = false,
}: ConfirmModalProps) {
  return (
    <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={onClose} title={title} size="sm">
      <p className="text-gray-600 mb-6">{message}</p>
      <div className="flex justify-end gap-3">
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          disabled={loading}
          className="btn btn-outline"
        >
          {cancelText}
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={onConfirm}
          disabled={loading}
          className={variant === "danger" ? "btn btn-danger" : "btn btn-primary"}
        >
          {loading ? "Processing..." : confirmText}
        </button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\shared\Select.tsx
============================================================

"use client";

import { forwardRef, SelectHTMLAttributes, OptionHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

interface SelectProps extends SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement> {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
  helperText?: string;
  options: Array<{ value: string | number; label: string }>;
  placeholder?: string;
}

export const Select = forwardRef<HTMLSelectElement, SelectProps>(
  ({ className, label, error, helperText, options, placeholder, id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const selectId = id || label?.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "-");

    return (
      <div className="w-full">
        {label && (
          <label htmlFor={selectId} className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1.5">
            {label}
          </label>
        )}
        <select
          ref={ref}
          id={selectId}
          className={clsx(
            "w-full rounded-lg border bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-gray-900 transition-all duration-200 appearance-none bg-[url('data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2224%22%20height%3D%2224%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2024%2024%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22%236b7280%22%20stroke-width%3D%222%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpolyline%20points%3D%226%209%2012%2015%2018%209%22%3E%3C%2Fpolyline%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E')] bg-[right_0.75rem_center] bg-no-repeat pr-10",
            error
              ? "border-danger-500 focus:border-danger-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-danger-500/20"
              : "border-gray-300 focus:border-primary-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500/20",
            className
          )}
          aria-invalid={error ? "true" : "false"}
          aria-describedby={error ? `${selectId}-error` : helperText ? `${selectId}-helper` : undefined}
          {...props}
        >
          {placeholder && (
            <option value="" disabled>
              {placeholder}
            </option>
          )}
          {options.map((option) => (
            <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
              {option.label}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        {error && (
          <p id={`${selectId}-error`} className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-danger-600" role="alert">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
        {helperText && !error && (
          <p id={`${selectId}-helper`} className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-gray-500">
            {helperText}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }
);

Select.displayName = "Select";

FILE: src\lib\auth.ts
============================================================

import { SignJWT, jwtVerify } from "jose";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";
import connectDB from "./mongodb";
import Admin from "@/models/Admin";

const COOKIE_NAME = process.env.ADMIN_COOKIE_NAME || "admin_session";

function getSecretKey() {
  const secret = process.env.ADMIN_SECRET_KEY;
  if (!secret || secret.length < 32) {
    throw new Error("ADMIN_SECRET_KEY must be at least 32 characters long");
  }
  return new TextEncoder().encode(secret);
}

function getCookieKey() {
  const password = process.env.ADMIN_COOKIE_PASSWORD;
  if (!password || password.length !== 32) {
    throw new Error("ADMIN_COOKIE_PASSWORD must be exactly 32 characters long");
  }
  return new TextEncoder().encode(password);
}

export interface AdminPayload {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  email: string;
  role: string;
  iat?: number;
  exp?: number;
}

export async function hashPassword(password: string): Promise<string> {
  return bcrypt.hash(password, 12);
}

export async function verifyPassword(password: string, hashedPassword: string): Promise<boolean> {
  return bcrypt.compare(password, hashedPassword);
}

export async function createToken(payload: AdminPayload): Promise<string> {
  return new SignJWT({ ...payload })
    .setProtectedHeader({ alg: "HS256" })
    .setIssuedAt()
    .setExpirationTime("24h")
    .sign(getSecretKey());
}

export async function verifyToken(token: string): Promise<AdminPayload | null> {
  try {
    const { payload } = await jwtVerify(token, getSecretKey());
    return payload as unknown as AdminPayload;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export async function getSession(): Promise<AdminPayload | null> {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const token = cookieStore.get(COOKIE_NAME)?.value;

  if (!token) return null;

  return verifyToken(token);
}

export async function setSession(payload: AdminPayload): Promise<void> {
  const token = await createToken(payload);
  const cookieStore = await cookies();

  cookieStore.set(COOKIE_NAME, token, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
    sameSite: "lax",
    maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24, // 24 hours
    path: "/",
  });
}

export async function clearSession(): Promise<void> {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  cookieStore.delete(COOKIE_NAME);
}

export async function requireAuth(): Promise<AdminPayload> {
  const session = await getSession();

  if (!session) {
    throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");
  }

  await connectDB();
  const admin = await Admin.findById(session.id).select("-passwordHash").lean();

  if (!admin || !admin.isActive) {
    await clearSession();
    throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED");
  }

  return session;
}

export async function requireRole(allowedRoles: string[]): Promise<AdminPayload> {
  const session = await requireAuth();

  if (!allowedRoles.includes(session.role)) {
    throw new Error("FORBIDDEN");
  }

  return session;
}

export const ROLES = {
  SUPER_ADMIN: "super_admin",
  ADMIN: "admin",
  EDITOR: "editor",
} as const;

// Shared helpers: market schedule, slugs, IST dates, auto results, monthly chart builder,
// case-insensitive market identity, and resilient cached fetching.

/* ------------------------------- constants ------------------------------- */

/** Default markets = team names (home daily table shows a row for each team). */
export const DEFAULT_MARKETS = teamNames;

/** Legacy: highlighting is 100% admin-controlled, so nothing is highlighted by default. */
export const MAJOR_SLUGS: string[] = [];

export const MONTH_NAMES = [
  "January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June",
  "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December",
];
export const MONTH_SHORT = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];
export const WEEKDAYS = ["Sun", "Mon", "Tue", "Wed", "Thu", "Fri", "Sat"];

/** Legacy grace window (kept for compatibility). */
export const GRACE_MINUTES = 10;
/** Draw time passed but no admin result. */
export const MISSING_MARK = "--";
/** Upcoming draw (time not reached). Set to "❌" if you prefer the emoji. */
export const UPCOMING_MARK = "XX";

/** LIVE = already drawn today; NEXT = upcoming within 6h; REST = later. */
export const NEXT_WINDOW_MINUTES = 360;
export type DrawSection = "LIVE" | "NEXT" | "REST";
export function drawSection(minutes: number, nowMinutes: number): DrawSection {
  if (nowMinutes >= minutes) return "LIVE";
  if (minutes - nowMinutes <= NEXT_WINDOW_MINUTES) return "NEXT";
  return "REST";
}

/* --------------------------- identity / naming --------------------------- */

export function slugify(name: string): string {
  return name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "");
}
export function titleFromSlug(slug: string): string {
  return slug.split("-").map((w) => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1)).join(" ");
}
/** Case-insensitive identity key: "Sadabahar" === "sadabahar". */
export function marketKey(name: string): string {
  return name.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
}
/** Canonical storage/display form: "sadabahar"/"SADABAHAR" -> "Sadabahar". */
export function canonicalMarketName(name: string): string {
  return name
    .trim()
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .split(" ")
    .map((w) => (w ? w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1).toLowerCase() : w))
    .join(" ");
}
/** Escape a string for safe use inside a MongoDB $regex. */
export function escapeRegExp(s: string): string {
  return s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
}

/* -------------------------------- drawing -------------------------------- */

/** Reference schedule exactly as shown on the reference site (24h HH:MM). */

/** Shift a whole schedule by N minutes (negative = earlier), wrapping across midnight. */
function shiftTimes(times: Record<string, string>, deltaMinutes: number): Record<string, string> {
  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const [slug, hhmm] of Object.entries(times)) {
    const [h, m] = hhmm.split(":").map(Number);
    let total = (h * 60 + m + deltaMinutes) % 1440;
    if (total < 0) total += 1440;
    out[slug] = `${String(Math.floor(total / 60)).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(total % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  }
  return out;
}

/** Reference schedule − 30 minutes, for legacy/reference-named markets. */
const DEFAULT_DRAW_TIMES: Record<string, string> = shiftTimes(REFERENCE_DRAW_TIMES, -30);

/** Team tables get the −30 min schedule positionally (alphabetical), preserving groupings. */
const TEAM_SCHEDULE: string[] = [
  "13:30", "13:30", "13:30", "13:30", "13:30", "13:30", "13:30",
  "13:25", "13:15", "12:45", "12:45",
  "13:40", "13:40", "13:40", "13:45", "13:45", "13:50",
  "14:00", "14:00", "14:00", "14:00", "14:10", "14:10", "14:10",
  "14:15", "14:30", "14:30", "14:30", "14:30", "14:30", "14:35",
  "14:40", "14:45", "14:45", "14:45", "15:00", "15:00", "15:15",
  "15:30", "15:35", "16:00", "16:05", "16:05", "16:30", "16:35",
  "16:40", "16:45", "17:00", "17:30", "17:30", "17:30", "17:30", "17:30",
  "18:45", "18:50", "19:00", "19:00", "19:00", "19:00", "19:05",
  "19:10", "19:10", "19:10", "19:15", "19:15", "19:25", "19:25",
  "19:30", "19:30", "19:30", "19:30", "19:30", "19:30", "19:30", "19:30",
  "19:35", "19:35",
  "19:45", "19:45", "19:45", "19:45", "19:45", "19:45", "19:45", "19:45",
  "19:50", "20:00", "20:00", "20:00", "20:05", "20:10",
  "20:15", "20:15", "20:20", "20:30", "20:35",
];

[...teamNames]
  .sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b))
  .forEach((name, i) => {
    DEFAULT_DRAW_TIMES[slugify(name)] = TEAM_SCHEDULE[i] ?? "12:00";
  });

/** Admin-set time wins; otherwise the market's scheduled default. */
export function getDrawTime(slug: string, dbTime?: string | null): string {
  if (dbTime && /^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/.test(dbTime)) return dbTime;
  return DEFAULT_DRAW_TIMES[slug] || "12:00";
}
export function getDefaultDrawTime(slug: string): string {
  return DEFAULT_DRAW_TIMES[slug] || "12:00";
}
export function minutesOf(hhmm: string): number {
  const [h, m] = hhmm.split(":").map(Number);
  return (h || 0) * 60 + (m || 0);
}
export function fmtTimeLabel(hhmm: string): string {
  const [h, m] = hhmm.split(":").map(Number);
  const period = h >= 12 ? "PM" : "AM";
  const h12 = h % 12 === 0 ? 12 : h % 12;
  return `${h12.toString().padStart(2, "0")}:${m.toString().padStart(2, "0")} ${period}`;
}

/* ------------------------------- IST dates ------------------------------- */

/** Current time in IST (UTC+5:30) regardless of visitor timezone. */
export function istNow(): Date {
  const now = new Date();
  const utc = now.getTime() + now.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000;
  return new Date(utc + 5.5 * 3600000);
}
export interface DayParts { day: number; month: number; year: number; }
export function istToday(now: Date): DayParts {
  return { day: now.getDate(), month: now.getMonth() + 1, year: now.getFullYear() };
}
export function addDays(p: DayParts, delta: number): DayParts {
  const d = new Date(p.year, p.month - 1, p.day + delta);
  return { day: d.getDate(), month: d.getMonth() + 1, year: d.getFullYear() };
}
export function weekdayOf(p: DayParts): string {
  return WEEKDAYS[new Date(p.year, p.month - 1, p.day).getDay()];
}
export function ordinal(n: number): string {
  const s = ["th", "st", "nd", "rd"];
  const v = n % 100;
  return `${n}${s[(v - 20) % 10] || s[v] || s[0]}`;
}
export function longDate(p: DayParts): string {
  return `${MONTH_NAMES[p.month - 1]} ${p.day}, ${p.year}`;
}
export function daysInMonth(y: number, m: number): number {
  return new Date(y, m, 0).getDate();
}
/** Comparable numeric date (y*10000 + m*100 + d). */
export function dateKey(y: number, m: number, d: number): number {
  return y * 10000 + m * 100 + d;
}

/* ------------------------------ auto results ----------------------------- */

/** Deterministic pseudo-random 2-digit value — backfill only, never today's reveal. */
export function autoResult(seedKey: string, day: number, month: number, year: number): string {
  const seed = `${seedKey}-${day}-${month}-${year}`;
  let hash = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < seed.length; i++) {
    hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + seed.charCodeAt(i);
    hash |= 0;
  }
  return (Math.abs(hash) % 100).toString().padStart(2, "0");
}

/** Earliest entry date per market slug = its "generation" date (as a dateKey). */
export function firstEntryDates(
  charts: Array<{ chartName: string; date: number; month: number; year: number }>
): Map<string, number> {
  const m = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const c of charts) {
    const s = slugify(c.chartName);
    const k = dateKey(c.year, c.month, c.date);
    const prev = m.get(s);
    if (prev === undefined || k < prev) m.set(s, k);
  }
  return m;
}

/* -------------------------------- taglines ------------------------------- */

/** Years used in the home tagline (2015 .. last year). */
export function taglineYears(): number[] {
  const cy = istNow().getFullYear();
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (let y = 2015; y < cy; y++) out.push(y);
  return out;
}
/** Earliest year the site covers — used by every selector, URL guard and validator. */
export const MIN_YEAR = 2015;

/** Year options for selectors (2015 .. current, newest first). */
export function yearOptions(): number[] {
  const cy = istNow().getFullYear();
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (let y = MIN_YEAR; y <= cy; y++) out.push(y);
  return out.reverse();
}
/* ------------------------------ monthly chart ---------------------------- */

export interface MonthChartLite {
  chartName: string;
  result: string;
  date: number;
  isHighlighted?: boolean;
  drawTime?: string | null;
}
export interface MonthlyRowData {
  date: number;
  values: Record<string, string>;
  red: boolean;
}

/**
 * Monthly rows: day 1 → today for the current month, full month for past months,
 * nothing for future months.
 *
 * Cell rules (first match wins):
 *   1. admin entry with a non-blank result   → that result
 *   2. past day + SPECIAL 4                  → auto 2-digit (always backfilled)
 *   3. past day + before market's generation → auto 2-digit
 *   4. past day + on/after generation        → "--"
 *   5. today                                 → "--" once draw time passed, else "XX"
 *   6. future day                            → "XX"
 *
 * Draw time used for today's gate: the TODAY entry's time wins; otherwise the
 * newest day's time within the selected month; otherwise the scheduled default.
 */
export function buildMonthlyRows(
  columns: Array<{ key: string }>,
  sel: { year: number; month: number },
  charts: MonthChartLite[],
  today: DayParts,
  nowMinutes: number,
  firstEntry: Map<string, number>,
  hidden: string[] = []
): MonthlyRowData[] {
  const hiddenSet = new Set(hidden);
  const byKey = new Map<string, MonthChartLite>();
  const timeBySlug = new Map<string, string>();
  const timeRank = new Map<string, number>();
  const isCurrentMonth = sel.year === today.year && sel.month === today.month;

  for (const c of charts) {
    const s = slugify(c.chartName);
    byKey.set(`${s}|${c.date}`, c);
    if (!c.drawTime) continue;
    const rank = isCurrentMonth && c.date === today.day ? 1_000_000 : c.date;
    const prev = timeRank.get(s);
    if (prev === undefined || rank > prev) {
      timeRank.set(s, rank);
      timeBySlug.set(s, c.drawTime);
    }
  }

  const dim = daysInMonth(sel.year, sel.month);
  const monthStart = new Date(sel.year, sel.month - 1, 1).getTime();
  const currentMonthStart = new Date(today.year, today.month - 1, 1).getTime();
  const lastDay = monthStart > currentMonthStart ? 0 : monthStart === currentMonthStart ? today.day : dim;
  const todayTime = new Date(today.year, today.month - 1, today.day).getTime();

  const rows: MonthlyRowData[] = [];
  for (let day = 1; day <= lastDay; day++) {
    const values: Record<string, string> = {};
    const cellTime = new Date(sel.year, sel.month - 1, day).getTime();
    const cellKey = dateKey(sel.year, sel.month, day);

    for (const col of columns) {
      const db = byKey.get(`${col.key}|${day}`);
      if (db && db.result && db.result.trim() !== "") {
        values[col.key] = db.result;
        continue;
      }

      // Blank / pending / no entry → backfill or placeholder rules.
      const isHidden = hiddenSet.has(col.key);
      const isSpecial = SPECIAL_SLUGS.includes(col.key) && !isHidden;
      const gen = firstEntry.get(col.key);
      const beforeGeneration = isHidden ? false : gen === undefined ? cellTime < todayTime : cellKey < gen;

      if (cellTime < todayTime) {
        values[col.key] =
          isSpecial || beforeGeneration
            ? autoResult(col.key, day, sel.month, sel.year)
            : MISSING_MARK;
      } else if (cellTime === todayTime) {
        const mins = minutesOf(getDrawTime(col.key, timeBySlug.get(col.key)));
        values[col.key] = nowMinutes >= mins ? MISSING_MARK : UPCOMING_MARK;
      } else {
        values[col.key] = UPCOMING_MARK;
      }
    }

    rows.push({ date: day, values, red: charts.some((c) => c.date === day && c.isHighlighted) });
  }
  return rows;
}

/* ------------------------- resilient cached fetching ------------------------ */

export interface CacheableChart {
  id: string;
  chartName: string;
  result: string;
  date: number;
  month: number;
  year: number;
  isHighlighted: boolean;
  isActive: boolean;
  drawTime?: string | null;
  updatedAt?: string;
}

const CACHE_KEY = "skf-charts-cache-v1";

const HIDDEN_CACHE_KEY = "skf-charts-hidden-v1";

function readCache(): Record<string, CacheableChart[]> {
  try {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return {};
    return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY) || "{}") as Record<string, CacheableChart[]>;
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

export function getCachedCharts(key: string): CacheableChart[] {
  try {
    return readCache()[key] || [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function readHiddenCache(): Record<string, string[]> {
  try {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return {};
    return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(HIDDEN_CACHE_KEY) || "{}") as Record<string, string[]>;
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}
export function getCachedHidden(key: string): string[] {
  try {
    return readHiddenCache()[key] || [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}
function setCachedHidden(key: string, slugs: string[]): void {
  try {
    if (typeof window === "undefined" || !Array.isArray(slugs)) return;
    const cache = readHiddenCache();
    cache[key] = slugs;
    localStorage.setItem(HIDDEN_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(cache));
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
}

/** A SUCCESSFUL response (even empty) is stored so deletions propagate; failed fetches never touch the cache. */
export function setCachedCharts(key: string, data: CacheableChart[]): void {
  try {
    if (typeof window === "undefined" || !Array.isArray(data)) return;
    const cache = readCache();
    cache[key] = data;
    localStorage.setItem(CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(cache));
  } catch {
    /* storage blocked/full – ignore */
  }
}

/** Fetch with retries. Returns null ONLY when every attempt failed → caller keeps previous data. */
export async function fetchChartsResilient(
  url: string,
  cacheKey: string,
  attempts = 4
): Promise<CacheableChart[] | null> {
  for (let i = 0; i < attempts; i++) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(url, { cache: "no-store" });
      if (res.ok) {
        const d = await res.json();
           if (d && d.success === true && Array.isArray(d.data)) {
          setCachedCharts(cacheKey, d.data);
          setCachedHidden(cacheKey, Array.isArray(d.hidden) ? (d.hidden as string[]) : []);
          return d.data as CacheableChart[];
        }
      }
    } catch {
      /* network error – retry */
    }
    if (i < attempts - 1) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000 * (i + 1)));
  }
  return null;
}

FILE: src\lib\mongodb.ts

import mongoose from "mongoose";

interface MongooseCache {
  conn: typeof mongoose | null;
  promise: Promise<typeof mongoose> | null;
}

declare global {
  var mongoose: MongooseCache | undefined;
}

let cached = global.mongoose;

if (!cached) {
  cached = global.mongoose = { conn: null, promise: null };
}

function getMongoUri(): string {
  const uri = process.env.MONGODB_URI;
  if (!uri) {
    throw new Error("Please define the MONGODB_URI environment variable inside .env.local");
  }
  return uri;
}

async function connectDB(): Promise<typeof mongoose> {
  if (cached!.conn) {
    return cached!.conn;
  }

  if (!cached!.promise) {
    const opts = {
      bufferCommands: false,
      maxPoolSize: 10,
      serverSelectionTimeoutMS: 5000,
      socketTimeoutMS: 45000,
    };

    cached!.promise = mongoose.connect(getMongoUri(), opts).then((mongoose) => {
      console.log("MongoDB connected successfully");
      return mongoose;
    });
  }

  try {
    cached!.conn = await cached!.promise;
  } catch (e) {
    cached!.promise = null;
    throw e;
  }

  return cached!.conn;
}

export default connectDB;

import { z } from "zod";
import { SPECIAL_SLUGS, slugify } from "./markets";

/* -------------------------------- constants ------------------------------- */

/** Earliest year the site covers (mirrors MIN_YEAR in markets.ts). */
const MIN_YEAR = 2015;
/** Latest allowed year — no future charts. */
const MAX_YEAR = new Date().getFullYear();

/* ---------------------------------- auth ---------------------------------- */

export const loginSchema = z.object({
  username: z.string().min(3, "Username must be at least 3 characters"),
  password: z.string().min(6, "Password must be at least 6 characters"),
});

/* --------------------------------- charts --------------------------------- */

/** Base schema (no date refine) — reused for partial updates. */
const chartBaseSchema = z.object({
  chartName: z.string().min(1, "Chart name is required").max(100, "Chart name too long"),

  // Blank is allowed: pre-create the table now, publish the number later.
  result: z.string().max(20, "Result too long").default(""),
  openingResult: z.string().max(20, "Opening result too long").optional().nullable(),
  closingResult: z.string().max(20, "Closing result too long").optional().nullable(),

  date: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(31, "Invalid date"),
  month: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(12, "Invalid month"),
  year: z.coerce
    .number()
    .int()
    .min(MIN_YEAR, `Year must be ${MIN_YEAR} or later`)
    .max(MAX_YEAR, `Year must be between ${MIN_YEAR} and ${MAX_YEAR}`),

  displayOrder: z.coerce.number().int().min(0).default(0),
  isHighlighted: z.boolean().default(false),
  isActive: z.boolean().default(true),

  scheduledAt: z
    .string()
    .optional()
    .nullable()
    .refine(
      (val) => {
        if (!val || val === "") return true; // empty/null allowed
        return !isNaN(new Date(val).getTime());
      },
      { message: "Invalid date time format", path: ["scheduledAt"] }
    ),

  drawTime: z
    .union([z.literal(""), z.string().regex(/^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/, "Invalid draw time (HH:MM)")])
    .optional()
    .nullable(),
});

/** Create schema — blocks future dates; past backfill only for the Special 4. */
export const chartSchema = chartBaseSchema.refine(
  (data) => {
    const today = new Date();
    today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    const selectedDate = new Date(data.year, data.month - 1, data.date);
    if (selectedDate > today) return false;
    if (selectedDate < today && !SPECIAL_SLUGS.includes(slugify(data.chartName))) return false;
    return true;
  },
  {
    message: "Future dates not allowed; past dates only for Gali, Desawar, Faridabad, Ghaziabad",
    path: ["date"],
  }
);

/** Update schema — no future/past restriction (editing an existing row). */
export const chartUpdateSchema = chartBaseSchema.partial();

/** List/filter schema for the admin charts API. */
export const chartFilterSchema = z.object({
  search: z.string().optional(),
  date: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(31).optional(),
  month: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(12).optional(),
  year: z.coerce.number().int().min(MIN_YEAR).max(MAX_YEAR).optional(),
  isActive: z.enum(["all", "true", "false"]).optional(),
  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).default(1),
  limit: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(100).default(20),
  sortBy: z.enum(["createdAt", "displayOrder", "date", "chartName"]).default("displayOrder"),
  sortOrder: z.enum(["asc", "desc"]).default("asc"),
});

/* --------------------------------- admins --------------------------------- */

export const adminSchema = z.object({
  username: z.string().min(3, "Username must be at least 3 characters").max(50),
  email: z.string().email("Invalid email address"),
  password: z.string().min(8, "Password must be at least 8 characters"),
  role: z.enum(["super_admin", "admin", "editor"]).default("admin"),
});

export const updateAdminSchema = adminSchema.partial().omit({ password: true }).extend({
  password: z.string().min(8).optional().or(z.literal("")),
  isActive: z.boolean().optional(),
});

/* ---------------------------------- types --------------------------------- */

export type LoginInput = z.infer<typeof loginSchema>;
export type ChartInput = z.infer<typeof chartSchema>;
export type ChartFilterInput = z.infer<typeof chartFilterSchema>;
export type AdminInput = z.infer<typeof adminSchema>;
export type UpdateAdminInput = z.infer<typeof updateAdminSchema>;

/* --------------------------------- helper --------------------------------- */

export function validateData<T>(
  schema: z.ZodSchema<T>,
  data: unknown
): { success: true; data: T } | { success: false; errors: z.ZodError } {
  const result = schema.safeParse(data);
  if (result.success) return { success: true, data: result.data };
  return { success: false, errors: result.error };
}

// src/middleware.ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { jwtVerify } from "jose";

export async function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;

  // Protect all /admin routes except the login page and API login
  if (
    pathname.startsWith("/admin") &&
    pathname !== "/admin/login" &&
    !pathname.startsWith("/api/admin/login")
  ) {
    const token = request.cookies.get("admin_session")?.value;

    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/admin/login", request.url));
    }

    try {
      // Verify the JWT token using the same secret used in src/lib/auth.ts
      const secret = new TextEncoder().encode(process.env.ADMIN_SECRET_KEY!);
      await jwtVerify(token, secret);
      
      // Token is valid, allow access
      return NextResponse.next();
    } catch (err) {
      // Token is invalid or expired, delete cookie and redirect to login
      const response = NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/admin/login", request.url));
      response.cookies.delete("admin_session");
      return response;
    }
  }

  return NextResponse.next();
}

// Only run middleware on admin routes
export const config = {
  matcher: ["/admin/:path*", "/api/admin/:path*"],
};

============================================================
FILE: src\models\Admin.ts
============================================================

import mongoose, { Document, Model, Schema } from "mongoose";
import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";

export interface IAdmin extends Document {
  username: string;
  email: string;
  passwordHash: string;
  role: "super_admin" | "admin" | "editor";
  isActive: boolean;
  lastLoginAt?: Date;
  loginAttempts: number;
  lockUntil?: Date;
  createdAt: Date;
  updatedAt: Date;
  isLocked: boolean;
  comparePassword(password: string): Promise<boolean>;
  incrementLoginAttempts(): Promise<void>;
  resetLoginAttempts(): Promise<void>;
}

const adminSchema = new Schema<IAdmin>(
  {
    username: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      unique: true,
      trim: true,
      minlength: 3,
      maxlength: 50,
    },
    email: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      unique: true,
      trim: true,
      lowercase: true,
    },
    passwordHash: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      select: false,
    },
    role: {
      type: String,
      enum: ["super_admin", "admin", "editor"],
      default: "admin",
    },
    isActive: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: true,
    },
    lastLoginAt: {
      type: Date,
    },
    loginAttempts: {
      type: Number,
      default: 0,
    },
    lockUntil: {
      type: Date,
    },
  },
  {
    timestamps: true,
  }
);

adminSchema.index({ username: 1 });
adminSchema.index({ email: 1 });
adminSchema.index({ isActive: 1 });

adminSchema.virtual("isLocked").get(function (this: IAdmin) {
  return !!(this.lockUntil && this.lockUntil > new Date());
});

adminSchema.methods.comparePassword = async function (password: string): Promise<boolean> {
  return bcrypt.compare(password, this.passwordHash);
};

adminSchema.methods.incrementLoginAttempts = async function (): Promise<void> {
  const maxAttempts = 5;
  const lockTime = 15 * 60 * 1000; // 15 minutes

  if (this.loginAttempts + 1 >= maxAttempts && !this.isLocked) {
    this.lockUntil = new Date(Date.now() + lockTime);
  }

  this.loginAttempts += 1;
  await this.save();
};

adminSchema.methods.resetLoginAttempts = async function (): Promise<void> {
  this.loginAttempts = 0;
  this.lockUntil = undefined;
  await this.save();
};

adminSchema.pre("save", async function (next) {
  if (!this.isModified("passwordHash")) return next();

  try {
    const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(12);
    this.passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash(this.passwordHash, salt);
    next();
  } catch (error) {
    next(error as Error);
  }
});

const Admin: Model<IAdmin> = mongoose.models.Admin || mongoose.model<IAdmin>("Admin", adminSchema);

export default Admin;

============================================================
FILE: src\models\Chart.ts
============================================================

import mongoose, { Document, Model, Schema } from "mongoose";

export interface IChart extends Document {
  chartName: string;
  result: string;
  openingResult?: string;
  closingResult?: string;
  date: number;
  month: number;
  year: number;
  displayOrder: number;
  isHighlighted: boolean;
  isActive: boolean;
  scheduledAt?: Date; // When the result should be published
  drawTime?: string;  // 24h "HH:MM" — admin-set reveal time
  createdAt: Date;
  updatedAt: Date;
  formattedDate: string;
}

const chartSchema = new Schema<IChart>(
  {
    chartName: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 100,
    },
    // Blank is allowed: pre-create the table now, publish the number later.
    result: {
      type: String,
      default: "",
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 20,
    },
    openingResult: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 20,
    },
    closingResult: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 20,
    },
    date: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 1,
      max: 31,
    },
    month: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 1,
      max: 12,
    },
      year: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 2015,
      max: 2030,
    },
    displayOrder: {
      type: Number,
      default: 0,
    },
    isHighlighted: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: false,
    },
    isActive: {
      type: Boolean,
      default: true,
    },
    scheduledAt: {
      type: Date,
    },
    drawTime: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
    },
  },
  {
    timestamps: true,
  }
);

chartSchema.index({ chartName: 1 });
chartSchema.index({ date: 1, month: 1, year: 1 });
chartSchema.index({ year: 1, month: 1, date: 1 });
chartSchema.index({ isActive: 1 });
chartSchema.index({ isHighlighted: 1 });
chartSchema.index({ displayOrder: 1 });
chartSchema.index({ chartName: 1, year: 1, month: 1, date: 1 }, { unique: true });

chartSchema.virtual("formattedDate").get(function (this: IChart) {
  return `${this.date.toString().padStart(2, "0")}/${this.month.toString().padStart(2, "0")}/${this.year}`;
});

chartSchema.set("toJSON", { virtuals: true });
chartSchema.set("toObject", { virtuals: true });

const Chart: Model<IChart> = mongoose.models.Chart || mongoose.model<IChart>("Chart", chartSchema);
export default Chart;
